- 					

An ancient hymn to Heracles, by Archilochus (fl. 650 BC), of which the first two lines were:

“Hail, O king Heracles, O victory-glorious!
							

	Hail thou and Iolaus, spear-victorious!”

It was traditionally sung in honour of the victor, whenever no special ode was ready. Its refrain, in imitation of the sound of striking lyre-strings, *τήνελλα καλλίνικε* (“cling-clang, O glorious victor!”) was thrice repeated. ↩︎

每当专门的颂歌尚未及备就，依传统便唱此歌以尊荣优胜者。其叠句摹拟琴弦拨击之声，作 *τήνελλα καλλίνικε*（“铮然作响，光荣的优胜者啊！”），连唱三遍。↩︎

## Introduction / 导言

===Original===
Though it is quite possible, and indeed probable, that the Epinician Odes do not represent Pindar’s highest achievement in the realm of pure poetry, the student of history has every reason to be thankful that, of all his works, it is these that have escaped the ruin that has overtaken the rest. For they furnish us, perhaps more fully and more convincingly than any other remains of antiquity, with a solution of a problem which has troubled historians. Some of these have exclaimed against what they consider the narrow, shortsighted, parochial spirit of the Greeks generally, in failing to see that their material prosperity, their safety and true independence would be best secured, if not by frankly enrolling themselves under the hegemony of one strong state (preferably Athens), at least by a close federation as binding on each member as that of the United States of America. Still more writers have denounced what they consider the unpatriotic selfishness of the aristocratical party in the several states, their unscrupulous plottings and alliances with the enemies of their respective cities, and, when they gained the upper hand, their ruthless treatment of the democracies.

Does not Pindar furnish us with the key to this jealous isolation, by showing us how each city cherished a belief in its divine origin, how the first parent of each state, or its founder, was a divine being, whose very name it perpetuated—for in Pindar the name of the goddess-founder and that of the state are interchangeable—Thebe, Aegina, Cyrene; or how its founders were demigods who settled there in obedience to divine commands? To subordinate this heavenly guardianship to other powers might well seem sacrilege. And the tradition of the sacredness of an independence thus hallowed, when once established, might well survive the age of unquestioning faith.

For the families of the aristocracy, to whom a thousand memories called far more powerfully and insistently than to any other order of nobility in the world’s history, there were incentives to political exclusiveness whose cogency we cannot appreciate unless we take the Olympian hierarchy as seriously as Pindar did. The chronique scandaleuse of Olympus, which shocked Plato, inspired Pindar. While repudiating belief in anything derogatory to the Gods, he does not consider their amours with mortal women (or even their paederasty) in that light. The men whose praises he sang had in their veins the blood of Gods: their human ancestors were half divine, and their achievements were worthy of their high descent. The sons of the great houses whose lineage was from Heracles, Aeacus, Perseus, counted themselves of different clay from the common herd: they recognised in their hearts that they owed no duty to a democratic constitution: they never ceased to chafe under the yoke of equality with beings whom they held scarce fit to be their servants, and to intrigue against a system which placed their personal liberty at the mercy of the caprice of the “base,” and allowed their wealth to be exploited for purposes with which they had no sympathy. No wonder that they accepted with complacency the poet’s digressions into the old heroic myths that to us are but fairy tales: for them these were unassailable fact. Their records in song were the charter of their superiority to the world around them, of their right divine to govern their fellow-men.

===Chinese===
尽管完全可能、甚至颇有可能，《竞技凯歌》（Epinician Odes）并不代表品达（Pindar）在纯粹诗歌王国中的最高成就，治史者却极有理由庆幸：在他全部的著作之中，得以免遭殃及其余诸作的覆亡之厄的，正是这批颂歌。因为它们为我们解答了一个久已困扰史家的难题，其详备与令人信服的程度，或许超过了古代遗存中的任何其他文献。有些史家曾痛斥他们所认为的希腊人普遍具有的狭隘、短视、偏守一隅的精神：希腊人竟未能看出，纵使不以坦然自附于某一强邦霸权之下的方式（最好是雅典〔Athens〕），至少也当以一个如美利坚合众国那般对各成员具有同等约束力的紧密联邦，来最稳妥地保障他们的物质繁荣、安全与真正的独立。更有为数更多的论者抨击他们所认为的各邦贵族党派不忠于邦国的自私自利：其肆无忌惮的阴谋密划、与各自城邦之敌的结盟，以及一旦得势之后对民主政体的残酷蹂躏。

品达岂非正为我们提供了理解这种猜忌的孤立状态的钥匙？他向我们昭示：每一城邦如何珍藏着自身源出神明的信仰；各邦的始祖——亦即其建邦者——如何是一位神祇，邦国永世奉守其名——因为在品达笔下，女神建邦者之名与城邦之名可以互代——忒拜（Thebe）、埃伊纳（Aegina）、昔兰尼（Cyrene）；或者，建邦者如何是奉神命而卜居其地的半神。将这种上天的护佑屈置于其他权力之下，很可能被视为渎神之举。而如此被神圣化的独立，其神圣性之传统一经确立，纵使在无所疑虑的信仰时代逝去之后，也极可能绵延不绝。

对于贵族世家而言——较之世界历史上任何其他贵族等级，千百种记忆对他们的召唤都更为有力、更为急切——政治上的排他自守自有其动因；除非我们像品达那样郑重看待奥林匹斯（Olympian）诸神的等序体系，否则便无从领会这些动因的效力。奥林匹斯山上那部令柏拉图（Plato）为之震骇的“丑闻编年史”（chronique scandaleuse），却使品达灵感泉涌。他虽然摈斥任何有损诸神的说法，却并不以那种眼光看待诸神与凡间女子的情爱（甚至他们对少年的眷爱）。他所歌颂的人们，血管中奔流着神明的血液：他们的人类祖先是半神，他们的功业亦无愧于高贵的世系。家世溯自赫拉克勒斯（Heracles）、埃阿科斯（Aeacus）、珀尔修斯（Perseus）的豪门子弟，自视与凡俗之辈非同一抔泥土所塑：他们心中明白，自己对民主政体不欠丝毫义务；与那些被他们看作用作仆役都嫌不配之辈同处平等，这副轭下他们从无一日不愤懑难平；而对于那种使其个人自由任凭“贱民”反复无常之心摆布、又使其财富被榨取以供他们毫无同感之用途的制度，他们的阴谋倾轧也从未止息。难怪他们心满意足地领受诗人旁骛于古老英雄神话的闲笔——那些神话在我们不过是童话，在他们却是无可攻讦的事实。这些载诸歌诗的功业记录，便是他们对周遭世界之优越地位的特许状，便是他们治理同胞之“神授权利”的特许状。

===Original===
It must also be remembered that these highborn men were superior to the lower classes not only in pride of lineage, but they bore about with them the witness to this in their bodily development. The aristocrat was a stronger man, and far more skilled in the use of that strength for personal encounters than the average democrat. He was a man of leisure, and we may say that practically all such men made it their aim, their daily practice, to perfect their physical condition in the gymnasiums which were in every town. In many states, perhaps in all which belonged to the Peloponnesian League, a small organized body of aristocrats kept the far more numerous commonalty in subjection, solely by their fighting superiority.1 We must not lose sight of the transcendent importance of bodily vigour in an age when all fighting was hand-to-hand, and where the numbers on each side were so small that a few abnormally strong men might, by breaking the enemies’ line, decide the issue. Each successful champion in the great athletic contests means a large number of men who went through a course of training in which they were wrought up to the highest pitch of perfection not only of muscular development, but also of pluck and endurance.

Among reasons which have been assigned for the discursiveness of these triumphal odes, in which a very small space is given to the victor, and none to the details of the contest, perhaps the most important was this: excessive praise was universally regarded as mischievous to its objects, as tending directly to provoke the jealousy of the Gods, and to invite their nemesis. In the mouth of an enemy it was malicious, tactless in that of a friend. Hence the praise in an ode was distributed widely: the bard celebrated the champion, his family, former victors, the city or island of his birth, his ancestors, the ancient heroes of the land, so that the *φθόνος* might fall heavily on none, especially as all success was ascribed to the Gods, to whom the competitors sacrificed before engaging in the contest. A religious halo surrounded the Great Games. “Not unto us” is the burden of more than one ode, in which success is expressly attributed to the help of a God, as Poseidon, and often to the Graces.

A feature of the Games which strikes us moderns unpleasantly is the absence among the Greeks of what we call the sporting spirit. While no praise was too high, no reward too splendid for the victor, his unsuccessful competitors neither expected nor obtained any sympathy even from their fellow-townsmen. Even the victor was sometimes assailed by envious disparagement, especially where a state was rent by political factions; while the vanquished had to hide his head from the storm of derision which greeted his failure.

===Chinese===
还须记住，这些出身高贵的人不仅在门第的自负上优于下层阶级，而且他们体格的发育本身便是随身携带的优越性之明证。贵族是更强壮的人，而且把这种膂力用于近身搏斗时，其娴熟程度远非寻常平民可比。他是有闲之人；我们可以说，这类人几乎无一不把在每座城镇皆设的体育馆（gymnasium）中完善体魄奉为目标与日课。在许多城邦——或许在隶属于伯罗奔尼撒同盟（Peloponnesian League）的全部城邦——一小支组织起来的贵族武装，单凭其战斗上的优势，便使人数远为众多的平民大众俯首臣服。1 我们切不可无视体魄之雄健在那种时代的超凡意义：其时一切战斗皆是白刃相接，双方人数又如此之少，以致数名异乎寻常的壮士便可能冲破敌阵而决出胜负。大赛之中每产生一名成功的优胜者，便意味着有大批的人经历过一整套训练；在那样的训练中，他们不仅肌肉之发达，连同胆气与坚忍，都被砥砺至尽善的极诣。

在这些庆捷颂歌之所以枝蔓旁骛——留给优胜者的篇幅极少，于竞赛的详情则绝口不提——人们曾举出种种缘由，其中最重要的或许是这样一条：过分的赞美被普遍认为有害于受赞之人，易于直接招致诸神的嫉恨，并引来它们的报应。此语出于仇敌之口便是恶毒，出于朋友之口便是冒昧失言。因此，颂歌中的赞美便广泛铺排：歌者既颂优胜者本人，亦颂其家族、昔日的优胜者、他诞生的城邦或岛屿、他的列祖列宗、故土的上古英雄，好让*φθόνος*（嫉恨）不致沉重地压到任何一人头上——尤其因为一切成功皆归于诸神，而竞技者赴赛之前都要向诸神献祭。神圣的光晕环绕着大赛会。“非归于我们”是不止一首颂歌的叠唱题旨：诗中把成功明白归之于某位神明的佑助——如波塞冬（Poseidon）——且时常归之于美惠女神（the Graces）。

赛会中有一特征令我们今人殊觉不快，那便是希腊人缺少我们所谓的“体育精神”（sporting spirit）。对优胜者而言，再高的赞誉不为过，再隆重的奖赏不为奢；而他那些失利的竞争者，纵在本邦同胞之间，也既不曾指望、亦不曾得到半点同情。甚至优胜者本人有时也会遭到出于嫉妒的贬损之词的攻讦，尤其在邦国为政治党争所撕裂之处；而落败者面对其失败所招致的讥嘲风暴，唯有掩首敛迹而已。

## The Isthmian Odes / 地峡颂

### I / 第一首

===Original===
For Herodotus of Thebes, on his victory in the chariot-race, BC 458 (?). The poet commences with an apology to Delos for making this ode take precedence of the completion of a paean to Apollo, which he was composing for the island of Keos (Paean IV).
===Chinese===
致忒拜（Thebes）的希罗多德（Herodotus），贺其四马战车赛夺冠，公元前 458 年（?）。诗人开篇向提洛（Delos）致歉，因为本颂抢在了他正为凯奥斯（Keos）岛创作的一首阿波罗（Apollo）颂完稿之前（即第四首派安颂，Paean IV）。

===Original===
Strophe 1

Mother mine, O Thebe of shield all-golden,
Me shall thy sovran behest embolden,
How full soever mine hands be, to lay
All other service aside for to-day.
O Delos, thou for whose exaltation
===Chinese===
正旋节 1

我的母亲，盾牌通体黄金的忒拜（Thebe）啊，
你至尊的谕令我必壮起胆量，
无论我双手的事务何等满盈，都要
把其余一切侍奉搁下，只为今天。
提洛啊，为了尊崇你，

===Original===
Hath my soul been outpoured, have no indignation!
What to a son true-hearted can be
More dear than a mother? Ah, yield to my plea,
Isle of Apollo! By grace of Heaven
Shall coupled fulfilment ere long be given
Unto hymnal-homages twain by me,
===Chinese===
我的灵魂早已倾洒，请莫要动怒！
对一颗赤诚的儿子之心，还有什么
比母亲更亲？啊，请应允我的恳求，
阿波罗的海岛！托上天的恩典，
不久便会有双份的圆满一同赐下——
由我把两份颂神的礼赞一并献上；

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

When to Him of the hair unshorn I come paying
Due honour with choral dance-arraying
In Keos by sea-waves weltered about—
Strains hailed by her shipmen with jubilant shout—
And honour the Isthmian ridge that doth sunder
===Chinese===
反旋节 1

待我前去向那位长发未剪的神明，
以合歌队排布的舞蹈献上应尽的敬意，
在四面海涛翻卷的凯奥斯岛上——
岛上的船夫将欢呼着迎候我的歌调——
我也要礼赞那道隔开两海的地峡山脊，

===Original===
Two seas that against its crag-walls thunder.
To Kadmus' people from Isthmus have gone
Six crowns in her athlete-contests won
To grace with triumphant victory's glory
My motherland, where, as is told in story,
Of Alkmena was born that aweless son
===Chinese===
两面的海涛正对着它的峭壁轰然雷鸣。
从地峡传到卡德摩斯（Kadmus）族人手中的，
是竞技会上赢得的六顶冠冕，
好为我的母邦添上凯旋的荣光——
我的母邦，如古老的传说所讲，
阿尔克墨涅（Alkmena）在那里生下那位无所畏惧的儿子，

===Original===
Epode 1

At whom quaked Geryon's Hounds, that never had quaked before.
For Herodotus frame I an honour-lay, for his four-horse team,
And the reins that himself swayed, needing none other man's chariot-lore.
I will sing so that he as a Kastor or Iolaus shall seem;
For these of all heroes were mightiest charioteers on earth.
Unto the one Lacedaemon, Thebes to the other gave birth.
===Chinese===
收束节 1

见了他，革律翁（Geryon）的猎犬也要战栗——往日它们何曾颤抖过。
我为希罗多德谱写这支荣耀之歌，为他的四马战车，
为他亲手执掌的缰绳——无需旁人传授御车的技艺。
我要放声歌唱，让他宛如卡斯托尔（Kastor），又似伊俄拉俄斯（Iolaus）；
因为在群英雄之中，唯有这二位是人间最雄健的御者。
一位由拉栖代梦（Lacedaemon）所生，另一位是忒拜的血脉。

===Original===
Strophe 2

More athlete-contests did these adventure
Than any of champions beside dared enter,
And with brazen tripods their halls they graced,
And with cauldrons and goblets of gold rich-chased;
For they tasted the rapture of strife victorious,
===Chinese===
正旋节 2

这二位闯过的竞技，比任何别的斗士
敢于尝试的都要多；
他们以青铜三足鼎装点厅堂，
又以精雕细錾的金釜金杯增辉；
因为他们尝过胜利争战的狂喜，

===Original===
And they bore thence garlands of triumph glorious;
And ever their prowess shone clear and bright,
Alike in the course where in eagle-flight
Raced runners with vestureless limbs white-flashing,
And when with the shields on their shoulders clashing
Men ran arrayed in the harness of fight,
===Chinese===
又从那里捧回凯旋的荣耀花环；
他们的英武始终闪耀着清澈的光辉，
无论是在那赛跑场上——健儿们
赤裸的肢体白亮如鹰隼疾飞，
还是肩上的盾牌铿然相撞之时——
披挂着战甲的汉子们奔跑竞逐，

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

And in all the deeds of their hands—in hurling
The javelin, and when they sped far-whirling
Across the field the discus of stone:—
For as yet was no fivefold contest known;
But each of the several strifes was striven
===Chinese===
反旋节 2

还有他们双手的一切技艺——掷出的
标枪，以及他们远远旋转着
掠过场地的石制铁饼：——
因为那时五项全能尚未问世；
各项比赛都是

===Original===
By itself, and to each was its own prize given.
So, many a time and oft, their hair
Wreathed with the victory-garlands fair,
These twain where Dirke's fount upleapeth,
Or where Eurotas' swift flood sweepeth,
Bowed thanking the nurturing waters there,
===Chinese===
单独举行，各有各的奖品。
因此，一回又一回，他们的头发
缠着美丽的胜利花冠，
这二位在狄尔刻（Dirke）泉水涌跳之处，
或在欧罗塔斯（Eurotas）急流奔涌的岸边，
俯身拜谢那滋养人的河水，

===Original===
Epode 2

By Dirke, Iphikles' son, his descent from the Dragon who drew;
By Eurotas, Tyndareus' scion, who dwelt the Achaians among,
In his highland home of Therapnae. And now farewell unto you!
O'er Poseidon and holy Isthmus I cast the mantle of song,
And over Onchestus' shores; and as this man's honours I tell,
I will sing of the fate to Asopodorus his sire that befell.
===Chinese===
收束节 2

在狄尔刻河畔，伊菲克勒斯（Iphikles）之子追叙自己是龙裔的血脉；
在欧罗塔斯河边，廷达瑞俄斯（Tyndareus）的后裔——他住在阿开奥斯（Achaians）人中间，
家在高原的特拉普涅（Therapnae）。如今且向你们二位道别！
我把歌的斗篷抛向波塞冬（Poseidon）与神圣的地峡，
也抛向翁刻斯托斯（Onchestus）的湖岸；当我传唱此人的荣耀，
也要歌咏降临在他父亲阿索波多罗斯（Asopodorus）身上的命运。

===Original===
Strophe 3

And Orchomenus' fields in my lay shall be chanted,
Henceforth by his father's memory haunted,
Who was cast on her strand, a shipwrecked wight,
From the boundless waters, in evil plight;
But with welcoming kindness that land embraced him.
===Chinese===
正旋节 3

我的歌中还要咏唱俄耳科墨诺斯（Orchomenus）的田野，
从今往后，那里萦绕着他父亲的记忆——
老人曾遭海难，被抛上那片滩头，
从无边的波涛中脱身，境况凄惨；
但那片土地以欢迎的厚意将他拥抱。

===Original===
Yet his house's fortune hath now upraised him
To behold once more the unclouded ray
Of prosperity's sun of the former day.
Yea, he who hath suffered sore tribulation
Wins forethought for pain's one compensation.
And bears it thenceforth in his heart for aye.
===Chinese===
而今家门的时运又把他高高托起，
让他重见昔日那轮昌盛的太阳
洒下的晴朗无云的光芒。
是啊，饱经忧患的人
赢得了深谋远虑——那是痛苦唯一的补偿，
从此永远铭记在心间。

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

If a man seek noble achievement's attaining,
With his soul's full energies upward straining,
Unsparing alike of cost and pains,
Meet is it that when at the last he gains
The prize, our ennobling praise he inherit
===Chinese===
反旋节 3

若有人立志成就高尚的功业，
灵魂的全部力量都向上奋争，
耗费钱财与辛劳都在所不惜，
那么，当他终于赢得
奖赏，让他承受我们高贵的赞美，

===Original===
Lavished on him with ungrudging spirit.
For easy it is for the bard inspired,
When by hard toil won is the goal desired,
To acclaim his endeavours with glad laudation,
And, along with the man, that the fame of his nation
Be set on high to be world-admired.
===Chinese===
慷慨无私地泼洒在他身上，才合乎公道。
因为受灵感鼓舞的歌人，尽可以轻松地
在艰难的汗水换来梦寐的标的后，
欢然称颂他的奋勉，
并且连同他本人，把他氏族的声名
一并高举，让世人仰慕。

===Original===
Epode 3

Sweet unto diverse men is the meed that from labour they reap,
To the shepherd, the ploughman, the fowler, to him who is fed from the sea.
Yet of these each strives but the wolf of hunger at bay to keep;
But who wins in the Games renown, or in battle victory,
When all men extol his achievement, receiveth the highest gain,
For praises as flowers on his head do strangers and citizens rain.
===Chinese===
收束节 3

各色人等从劳作中收获的酬报各有其甘甜——
牧人、耕夫、捕鸟人，还有以海为食的人。
然而这些人奋争，只为把饥饿的豺狼挡在门外；
而在赛会上赢得荣名、在沙场夺得胜利的人，
当万众称颂他的功业，便得到了至高的报偿，
因为异邦人与本邦人的赞美如繁花落满他的头顶。

===Original===
Strophe 4

O, well it beseemeth our lips, the awaking
Of thanksgiving-praise to the King earth-shaking,
Who is also our neighbour, Kronos' son,
He who sped of his kindness our chariots on,
Who is God of the swift steed goalward racing.
===Chinese===
正旋节 4

啊，我们的唇舌正宜唤醒
对那位震撼大地的君王的感恩颂赞——
他也是我们的近邻，克洛诺斯（Kronos）之子，
曾以恩慈助我们的战车疾驰，
他是骏马向终点飞驰之神。

===Original===
Meet is it withal that our song be praising,
Amphitryon, those great sons of thine,
And the Minyan valley's recess divine,
And Eleusis' Grove world-celebrated
To the Goddess Demeter consecrated,
And Euboea's course's curving line.
===Chinese===
我们的歌同样应当称颂，
安菲特律翁（Amphitryon）啊，你那两位伟大的儿子，
还有米尼亚（Minyan）人山谷中神圣的幽境，
以及厄琉西斯（Eleusis）举世闻名的圣林——
它献给了女神得墨忒耳（Demeter）——
还有优卑亚（Euboea）赛道的蜿蜒曲线。

===Original===
Antistrophe 4

And with these I acclaim, as in holy paean,
Thy sacred precinct by heroes Achaean
Reared, Protesilaus, in Phylake.
But to tell over every victory
Which Hermes the Lord of the Games hath given
===Chinese===
反旋节 4

在这一切之中，我还要如唱圣洁的派安颂一般，
称赞你的圣域——普罗忒西拉奥斯（Protesilaus）啊，
那是阿开奥斯的英雄们在弗拉刻（Phylake）筑起的。
但要把赛会之主赫尔墨斯（Hermes）
赐给那些骏马的每一场胜利

===Original===
To the steeds that in many a race have striven
To win for Herodotus triumph's bay,
The narrowing limits of this my lay
Take from me. Yea, and often the keeping
Of silence bringeth a richer reaping
Of joy, seeing Envy is balked of her prey.
===Chinese===
——它们身经百赛——
为希罗多德赢回凯旋的月桂——
我这支颂歌狭窄的疆界
不容许我如此。是啊，缄默不语，
常常能收获更丰盈的
喜乐，因为嫉妒从此扑空。

===Original===
Epode 4

Upborne on the shining wings of the sweet-voiced Muses nine,
With garlands from Pytho, with choicest wreaths from Alpheus' flood
And Olympia's contests won, may he his hands entwine
For the honour of Thebes seven-gated. But if one secretly brood
Over hoarded wealth, and at other men mouth, he considereth not
That to death he is rendering up his soul—and his name shall rot.
===Chinese===
收束节 4

乘着九位妙音缪斯（Muses）闪亮的翅膀高高飞翔，
愿他十指相缠，戴上来自皮托（Pytho）的花环、来自阿尔甫斯（Alpheus）河
与奥林匹亚（Olympia）赛会的最精美冠冕，
为七门之城忒拜增光。但若有人暗自盘算
着囤积的财货，对他人恶语相向，他不会想到
自己正把灵魂交予死亡——他的名声也将腐烂。

### II / 第二首

===Original===
For Xenokrates of Akragas, and his son Thrasybulus, on the victory in the chariot-race won by their charioteer Nikomachus, BC 472 (?). The ode was composed after the death of Xenokrates, and hence is addressed to his son.
===Chinese===
致阿克拉加斯（Akragas）的色诺克拉底（Xenokrates）及其子特拉叙布卢斯（Thrasybulus），贺其御者尼科马科斯（Nikomachus）夺得四马战车赛胜利，公元前 472 年（?）。本颂作于色诺克拉底去世之后，故致予其子。

===Original===
Strophe 1

The singers of old, Thrasybulus, who mounted the car of the Queens of Song,
The golden-tired, giving voice to the ringing lyre and the tuneful tongue,
Shot lightly the arrows of honey-sweet strains in the fair one's praise,
Whosoever by bright summer-bloom of lovely form and face
Stirred hearts to dream upon splendour-throned Aphrodite's grace.
===Chinese===
正旋节 1

特拉叙布卢斯啊，昔日的歌者们驾起歌之女王们的战车——
她们金饰盛装——拨响清越的竖琴，唱出婉转的歌喉，
轻轻射出蜜一般甘美的曲调之箭，赞颂那美丽的佳人——
无论谁，凭那可爱身姿与容颜盛放的夏花般光艳，
撩动人心去梦想端坐辉煌宝座的阿佛洛狄忒（Aphrodite）的恩宠。

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

For then was the Muse not yet a lover of gain, nor a hireling was she.
Nor then honey-throated Terpsichore sold the melting melody
Of her lays, nor with faces silver-masked did they tread the stage.
But now she biddeth us heed the word of the Argive sage
Which cometh all too near to the truth in this our age:
===Chinese===
反旋节 1

因为那时缪斯（Muse）尚不贪恋钱财，也不是雇来的佣工。
蜜喉的忒尔普西科瑞（Terpsichore）那时还不曾出卖她歌调中
融化的甜美旋律，歌者们也不曾蒙着银白的假面上场。
而如今她叮嘱我们留意那位阿尔戈斯（Argos）智者的话——
在我们这个时代，它实在太近乎实情：

===Original===
Epode 1

“ *'Tis money, 'tis money that maketh the man!*” he said,
When his friends forsook him so soon as his wealth had fled.
But enough—thou art wise. O, famous afar
Is the Isthmian victory won by the car
Thy swift steeds drew, that I sing.
For Poseidon gave to thy sire renown,
And the Dorian garland, the parsley crown
O'er Xenokrates' hair did he fling.
===Chinese===
收束节 1

“ *是金钱，是金钱造就了人！*”——当他财帛一散，
朋友们旋即离弃他时，他如此说。
不过够了——你是明白人。啊，声名远播的，
是你那迅捷骏马所驾的战车
赢得的地峡胜利，正是我要歌唱的。
因为波塞冬把荣耀赐给你父亲，
把多里安（Dorian）的花环——那顶欧芹冠冕
抛落在色诺克拉底的发间。

===Original===
Strophe 2

And so did he honour the lord of the goodly chariot, Akragas' star.
And at Krisa looked down on him graciously Apollo prevailing afar,
And gave to him glory. In gleaming Athens did he attain
Mid the sons of Erechtheus the grace of triumph; nor might he complain 20
Of the skill of the hands that lashed his horses and swayed the rein,
===Chinese===
正旋节 2

神明就这样礼敬那美善战车的主人、阿克拉加斯的明星。
在克里萨（Krisa），统御远方的阿波罗也慈祥地垂顾于他，
赐他荣光。在光辉的雅典（Athens），他在厄瑞克透斯（Erechtheus）的儿郎中间
赢得了凯旋的恩宠；那挥鞭策马、执辔驭马的手的技艺，20
他也无可抱怨，

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

Nikomachus' hands, that gave to his steeds full rein at the moment due,
He whom the truce-bearing heralds Elean of Zeus Kronion knew,
Who publish the Season of Games; for his hospitality well
They remembered; and sweetly their voices proclaimed o'er the hallowed dell
His triumph, when he on the lap of golden Victory fell
===Chinese===
反旋节 2

那正是尼科马科斯的手，懂得在合宜的时刻放松缰绳，
克洛诺斯之子宙斯麾下持休战和约的伊利斯（Elis）传令官都认得他——
正是他们宣告赛会的节期；他的盛情款待，
他们铭记于心；他的凯旋，他们甜美的声音在圣谷上空
高声宣布，那时他倒进金色胜利女神的怀抱，

===Original===
Epode 2

In their land, which they name the Grove of Olympus' Lord,
Where the sons of Aenesidamus gained the award
Of honours whose memory aye is enscrolled.
For, O Thrasybulus, known from of old
To the halls of thine ancient line
Is the winsome charm of the song that leaps
From the lips, as on the procession sweeps
In triumph for victory—thine!
===Chinese===
收束节 2

在他们的国土——人们称之为奥林波斯之主的圣林——
埃涅西达穆斯（Aenesidamus）的儿郎们在那里赢得了
永镌史册的荣耀褒奖。
因为，特拉叙布卢斯啊，它久已为人所知——
在你古老家系的厅堂里——那支歌
从唇边跃出，何等迷人，
当凯旋的队伍
为那属于你的胜利浩荡行进！

===Original===
Strophe 3

For not uphillward nor steep is the path, if the bard is fain to guide
The feet of the praises of Helicon's Maids with famous men to abide.
May song's shaft sped from mine hand as far past all else fly
As in sweetness of spirit unto Xenokrates none came nigh.
Amidst of his townsmen ever a prince of courtesy,
===Chinese===
正旋节 3

因为，只要歌人乐于引路，那条路既无需仰攀，也不陡峭：
让赫利孔（Helicon）女神们赞美的脚步与名士同行。
愿我手中射出的歌之箭飞得远远越过一切，
正如论性情的甘美，无人能近色诺克拉底之肩。
在乡邻中间，他永远是温良有礼的君侯，

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

After the wont of the Panhellenes horse-rearing he fostered still:
He was constant at every feast of the Gods: no wind's breath blew so chill
On his guest-fain board as to make him furl his canvas-spread;
But far as the Phasis in summertide's gales the fame of him sped.
And in wintertide anchored his guest-renown in broad Nile's bed.26
===Chinese===
反旋节 3

他循着全希腊人养育骏马的风尚，始终热心扶持：
诸神的每一场宴飨他都恒久与会：无论何等寒风
吹向他乐迎嘉宾的餐桌，也从未令他收卷帆篷；
夏日的长风把他的声名一直送到法西斯（Phasis）河畔，
冬季里，他待客的英名又停泊在宽阔的尼罗河口。26

===Original===
Epode 3

What though the cravings of envy like veils bedim
The vision of many men's souls?—ah, never let him
Hush into coward silence the praise
Of his father's prowess, nor these my lays!
Not statue-like idly to stand
Did I fashion them! Nikesippus, bear
This, to my loyal friend to declare,
When thou comest to that far land.
===Chinese===
收束节 3

嫉妒的贪念纵然像帷幔遮暗了
许多人的心灵之眼——啊，也千万别让他
怯懦地缄口，不赞美
父亲的勇武，不唱我的这些歌！
我造就这些歌，可不是让它们
像雕像般呆立无用！尼基西波斯（Nikesippus）啊，
请把这话捎给我那位忠贞的朋友，
当你抵达那遥远的国土。
### III / 第三首

===Original===
For Melissus of Thebes, on his victory in the chariot-race at Nemea, BC 477 (?). This ode is a mere prelude to IV: it is in the same metre, and many scholars are of opinion that they were originally one.
===Chinese===
致忒拜的墨利索斯（Melissus），贺其于涅墨亚（Nemea）四马战车赛夺冠，公元前 477 年（?）。本颂仅是第四首的前奏：两者格律相同，许多学者认为它们原为一首。

===Original===
Strophe

What man soever hath prospered in winning prizes of high renown
In the Games, or is mighty in wealth, who yet in his spirit crusheth down
Pestilent arrogance, worthy is he to be graced with his townsmen's praise;
For of thee, O Zeus, all excellence cometh that mortal men doth upraise;
And longer abideth their bliss who reverence thee: with the froward-hearted
Through life it abides not, but lo, as a suddenly vanishing dream hath departed.
===Chinese===
正旋节

无论是谁，若在赛会中屡屡赢得显赫的奖赏，
或是财力雄厚，却仍能在心中压制
恶性的骄狂，他便配得上乡邻的称誉；
因为宙斯（Zeus）啊，凡人身上一切使人昂扬的美德都出自你；
而敬奉你的人，福祉与之同住更久：对悖逆之心，
福乐终生不肯驻留，看哪，它已如倏忽而逝的梦飘然离去。

===Original===
Antistrophe

It beseems that in guerdon of glorious achievement the deeds of the valiant we sing;
It beseems that mid triumph-procession with grace of loving welcoming
Should our praises conspire to exalt him! In contests twain hath fortune fair
Favoured Melissus, to turn his heart to delightsome joy from care.
In the glens of the Isthmus he won for him crowns: where the thunder-throated lion
Prowled through the cavernous Nemean dell, he proclaimed him Thebe's scion
===Chinese===
反旋节

勇者的功业值得歌颂，作为辉煌成就的报偿；
在凯旋的队伍中，我们的赞美当同心协力，
以深情的礼遇将他高举！两度赛会，好运
都眷顾墨利索斯，化他心中的忧愁为甜美的欢欣。
在地峡的幽谷中他为自己赢得冠冕：在那雷喉的狮子
出没于洞窟森然的涅墨亚谷地之处，他宣告自己是忒拜的后裔

===Original===
Epode

In the chariot-contest triumphant. He bringeth
No stain on the mighty name
Of the prowess his sires made glorious
Of old. Well know ye the fame
Which Kleonymus won, as the old lay singeth
How his chariot raced victorious.
By the mother akin to the Labdakid Clan, they walked in the ways of wealth, and they trained
With manifold toil the yoke of four.
But time with its onward-rolling days bringeth change upon change: unscarred, unpained
Are none but the Gods' seed evermore.
===Chinese===
收束节

在战车赛中凯旋。他没有给
祖辈昔日以英武
铸就的威名
添上污点。你们都熟知克勒俄尼摩斯（Kleonymus）
赢得的名声，如古老的歌谣所唱，
他的战车如何胜利飞驰。
凭着母系与拉布达基德（Labdakid）家族的亲缘，他们行走于财富之途，又以
千般辛劳调驯四马的轭队。
然而时光滚滚向前，变化之上再生变化：唯有神明的后裔
才能永远无伤无痛。

### IV / 第四首

===Original===
For Melissus of Thebes, on his victory in the Pankration at the Isthmus, probably in the year preceding the chariot-victory at Nemea.
===Chinese===
致忒拜的墨利索斯，贺其于地峡全能搏击赛（Pankration）夺冠，约在涅墨亚战车赛获胜的前一年。

===Original===
Strophe 1

By grace of the Gods there be countless paths far-spreading before my feet;
But, Melissus, thou at the Isthmian Games hast shown me a highway meet
Whereon to follow in song the track of the prowess of thy line
Wherein the sons of Kleonymus ever have prospered by help divine,
And so pass on to the term of mortal life; but ever shifting
Are the winds of fate that swoop upon man, and drive him chartless-drifting.
===Chinese===
正旋节 1

托诸神的恩典，无数条道路在我脚下向四方铺展；
但墨利索斯啊，你在地峡赛会上为我指出一条合宜的康庄大道，
让我的歌得以循着你家系英武的足迹前行——
克勒俄尼摩斯的儿郎们在那条路上始终蒙神助而昌盛，
如此走过凡人生命的全程；然而命运的狂风
不断变向，扑向世人，把他驱入无图可循的漂泊。

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

Ay, the story of these from of yore is told, how with honour in Thebes they were named.
Warders they were of the tribes dwelling round, and in arrogance brawling unshamed
No part they had; and what records soever there be of the men which have died,
Or of yet living men, such as fly wind-blown through the whole world far and wide,
Records of limitless glory, these they attained in their fullness receiving:
Yea, Heracles' Pillars they touched by the gallant deeds of their line's achieving;—
===Chinese===
反旋节 1

是啊，他们的故事自古传述：他们如何荣名赫赫于忒拜。
他们是环居诸部落的守卫者，而狂傲无耻的争吵斗殴
与他们无涉；凡有关亡故之人
或当世之人的种种传闻——它们随风飞遍普天之下——
那无尽荣耀的篇章，他们都圆满赢得、悉数领受：
是啊，凭着家系英勇的功业，他们触摸到了赫拉克勒斯（Heracles）之柱；—

===Original===
Epode 1

But let none press on to achievement that reacheth
Farther beyond that bound!—
And in that house many a lover
Of the rearing of steeds was found.
And they joyed in the lore that the War-god teacheth.
But ere one day's hours passed over,
The merciless sleet-laden tempest of war had bereft of hero-scions four
That hearth once happy; but now again
After the wintry gloom of the months of changeful vesture, the earth once more
Hath blossomed with roses of crimson grain
===Chinese===
收束节 1

但愿无人企图把功业
推到那界限之外更远的地方！——
而在那户人家里，曾有众多
酷爱养马育驹的人。
战神传授的武艺也令他们欢喜。
然而还不到一日的时辰，
战争那挟着雨雹的风暴毫不容情，已把四位英雄子弟
从那曾经幸福的炉边夺去；但如今，
度过衣装屡变的月份那冬日的阴沉，大地又一次
绽开出殷红石榴色的玫瑰，

===Original===
Strophe 2

By the will of Heaven. The Shaker of Earth, who hath at Onchestus his halls,
And whose mansion is on the sea-lashed ridge afront of Corinth's walls,
Even he bestoweth upon that house this hymn of wondrous praise,
And the olden glory of far-famed deeds from her bed doth he upraise
Where erst she had fallen on sleep, but now is awakened, and shines resplendent
Over all her form, as the Morning Star mid the stars is a light transscendent—
===Chinese===
正旋节 2

这是上天的旨意。震撼大地者在翁刻斯托斯拥有宫邸，
在科林斯（Corinth）城墙前那海涛拍击的山脊上另有华堂，
正是他把这支奇妙赞美的颂歌赐予那户人家，
把往昔显赫功业的古老荣光从她的床榻上扶起——
她曾在那里酣眠，如今已然苏醒，周身
光辉灿烂，恰如群里之中那超凡的启明星——

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

That olden glory which even on Athens' fields proclaimed the renown
Of a chariot-triumph; then in Adrastus' Games at Sikyon-town
Gave wreathed leaves of such old-time song as these of our own days are.
Nor yet at the national contests failed they to ride the curved car;
But they joyed in contending with all the Hellenes, in spending on steeds their treasure.
But unhonoured, unsung, is the man that spareth his might against others to measure.
===Chinese===
反旋节 2

那古老的荣光甚至在雅典的赛场上也昭示过
战车凯旋的声名；其后在西锡安（Sikyon）城中
阿德拉斯托斯（Adrastus）的赛会上，编结了古歌的叶冠，一如我们今日的歌。
在全民族的竞技会上，他们也从未缺席弯形战车的驰骋；
他们乐于与全希腊人争胜，乐于把钱财花在骏马身上。
而那吝惜膂力、不肯与人对较量的人，无人礼敬，无人歌咏。

===Original===
Epode 2

Yea, even when champions in strife be contending,
Till the end in uncertainty
Her face Queen Fortune veileth;
For triumph now giveth she
And anon defeat; but at whiles in the ending
The craft of the weaker prevaileth
To cast to the earth the stronger. Ye know of the prowess of Aias the blood-stained fame,
How, when the night was now far spent,
He cast himself on his own sword, thereby bringing reproach and abiding shame
On the sons of Hellenes that Troy ward went.
===Chinese===
收束节 2

是啊，甚至在斗士们方酣相搏之际，
直到终局未分之前，
命运女王始终以面纱遮着脸；
她忽而赐予凯旋，
忽而又赐予败北；但有时到头来，
弱者的机巧反倒占了上风，
把更强者击倒在地。你们知道埃阿斯（Aias）的英武，那染血的名声——
当夜色已经深沉，
他扑向自己的剑锋，从此把责难与长久的耻辱
留给了远征特洛伊的希腊人的儿郎。

===Original===
Strophe 3

But lo, he is honoured of Homer the wide world over, who set on high
All knightly prowess of Aias; and his god-gifted poesy
Hath taught the measure whereby all other bards must frame the lay:
For a noble song passeth down the years with a voice that liveth for aye,
And over the harvest-abounding earth, and across the sea for ever
Goeth the sunbright shining of noble deeds, to be quenchèd never.
===Chinese===
正旋节 3

看哪，荷马（Homer）使他举世受尊荣——正是荷马把
埃阿斯的全部骑士般的英武高高举起；他那神授的诗艺
教给了格律，此后一切歌人都须依它谱制歌章：
因为高贵的歌带着永生的声音岁岁相传，
在丰获遍野的大地上、在辽阔海洋上，永远
行进着高贵功业那太阳般的光辉，永不熄灭。

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

May the Muses to us be gracious, that so we may kindle a beacon-light
Of song for Melissus, a wreath that shall worthily crown the pankratian might
Of the son of the House of Telesias; for he showeth in conflict's toil
The courage of roaring lions, and coupled therewith the fox's guile
Who lies on his back,27 so holding at bay the eagle's swoop down-rushing.
So cunning and strength must alike be used for the adversary's crushing.
===Chinese===
反旋节 3

愿缪斯垂恩于我们，好教我们为墨利索斯点燃
一座歌的灯塔，编一顶无愧于忒勒西阿斯（Telesias）家之子的
全能搏击伟力的花冠；因为他在鏖战的艰辛中显出
咆哮雄狮的胆魄，又兼具狐狸的诡黠——
那狐狸仰身贴地，却挡住苍鹰俯冲的扑击。27
要碾碎敌手，机巧与勇力必须并用。

===Original===
Epode 3

For not by nature was this man dowered
With Orion's giant height;
Mean was he to outward showing,
But with iron-heavy might
In the grapple his foe he overpowered.
So of old for Antaius' o'erthrowing
To his dwelling in Libya's corn-land came a man low-statured, a hero who bore
A spirit unflinching in conflict-strain,
A scion of Thebes whose wrestling-grip should for ever stay him from roofing o'er
With the skulls of strangers Poseidon's fane,
===Chinese===
收束节 3

因为造化并未赋予这人
俄里翁（Orion）那巨人的身量；
他外貌平平，
搏抱之中却以铁石般的
沉重伟力压服了他的对手。
古时为了推翻安泰俄斯（Antaius），
一位身材不高的英雄来到利比亚（Libya）麦乡中那怪的家宅，他怀着
临阵毫不动摇的精神——
那忒拜子弟的摔跤铁腕，使他从此永远不能再
用外乡人的头骨为波塞冬的神庙覆顶，

===Original===
Strophe 4

Even the son of Alkmena, who passed to Olympus after that he
Had tracked all lands, and traversed the cliff-walled face of the surf-white sea,
Had slain the sea-rovers, and safe for voyagers made the sea's highway.
And now by the Aegis-bearer in glorious bliss he dwelleth for aye,
As a friend is honoured of all the Immortals, with Hebe hath made affiance,
Is lord of a golden palace, is kinsman to Hera by spousal-alliance.
===Chinese===
正旋节 4

那正是阿尔克墨涅之子，他在走遍万国之后升上奥林波斯——
既已徒步历遍大地，又横渡白沫如雪、峭壁夹峙的沧海，
既已剿灭海寇，让航行人安然行于海上通途。
如今他在持神盾者的国度里永享辉煌的福祉，
受全体不死者以挚友之礼相敬，与赫柏（Hebe）缔订婚约，
是黄金宫殿的主人，因联姻而与赫拉（Hera）结成姻亲。

===Original===
Antistrophe 4

For him above the Elektran Gate we burghers the feast prepare,
And the crown-like ring of the altars newly-built will we set to him there,
And our sacrifices will offer for those eight bronze-mailed heroes who died,
Whom Megara, Kreon's daughter, bare, that mighty Hero's bride—
They unto whom at the sun's down-going the nightlong flame is uplifted,
And with odorous reek its smoke is lashing the welkin, through cloudland drifted.
===Chinese===
反旋节 4

我们城中人为他在厄勒克特拉门（Elektran Gate）上方备设圣宴，
还要把新建祭坛那花冠般的环列在那里为他陈设，
并为那八位青铜铠甲、早已捐躯的英雄献上祭品——
克瑞翁（Kreon）之女、那位伟力英雄的新娘墨伽拉（Megara）所生的儿郎——
每当日落，彻夜不熄的火焰便为他们高高举起，
香烟馥郁，烟柱抽打着天穹，在云国间飘荡。

===Original===
Epode 4

Then on the second day is holden
The struggle of athlete-might,
The crown of the year's games ended.
There with his brows made bright
With leaves of the myrtle-wreath enfolden
Twin triumphs Melissus blended,
When already among the boys he had won another by heeding diligently
The words of the wisely-counselling tongue
Of the pilot trainer who steered his course: with Melissus Orseas' name join I
As I shed on them grace of delightsome song.
===Chinese===
收束节 4

到第二天举行的，
便是力士们的搏击之赛，
一年赛会压轴的冠冕。
在那里，墨利索斯以桃金娘
花环的叶片缠裹眉额、熠熠生辉，
把两场凯旋合于一身；
此前他已在少年组赢得另一顶，只因他勤勉听从
那为他掌舵引路的导师
智慧诤言的叮咛：我把俄尔塞阿斯（Orseas）之名与墨利索斯相连，
把甜美颂歌的恩泽洒在他们身上。

### V / 第五首

===Original===
For Phylakidas of Aegina, son of Lampon, and brother of Pytheas (celebrated in the Fifth Nemean), on his victory in the Pankration, BC 476 (?).
===Chinese===
致埃吉那（Aegina）的弗拉基达斯（Phylakidas）——兰蓬（Lampon）之子、皮忒阿斯（Pytheas）之弟（《涅墨亚颂》第五首有颂），贺其全能搏击赛夺冠，公元前 476 年（?）。

===Original===
Strophe 1

Theia of many names, O mother of the Sun,28
Men set their stamp on gold for love of thee,
Of all things precious counting this the mightiest one;
Yea, and in rivalry,
Queen, for thy brightness on the sea do galleys clash in wars,
And in the whirling fight are marvels wrought by battle-cars.
===Chinese===
正旋节 1

众名之主忒亚（Theia）啊，太阳的母亲，28
世人因爱你在黄金上钤盖印记，
在一切珍宝中把它奉为最有力者；
是啊，而且在竞逐之中，
女王啊，为了你的光辉，战船在海上交锋激战，
旋卷的厮杀里，战车创下件件奇观。

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

He in the contests of the Games achieves renown
Desired of all, who hath won victory's meed
By hands that wreathed his head with many a crown,
Or by his fleet foot's speed.
'Tis Heaven awards each prize of strength: two things alone there be
That make life's loveliest blossoms blow in wealth's flower-spangled lea,
===Chinese===
反旋节 1

在赛会的竞技中赢得人人
渴慕的荣名的，是那以双手——曾为他的额头
编结过重重冠冕的双手——
或以神行的健足摘取胜利酬报的人。
每一项膂力的奖品都由上天颁授：唯有两件事，
能让生命最可爱的花朵开遍财富繁花点点的原野：

===Original===
Epode 1

To have good hap and reputation fair.
Seek not to be as Zeus; all things are thine
If to thee falls of these best gifts thy share;
For mortal bounds must mortal men confine.
But, O Phylakidas, for thee at Isthmus lies in store
A twofold meed of fadeless fame, at Nemea for you twain,
For thee and Pytheas, crowns pankratian. Oh, mine heart no more
Rapture of song can taste, except the Aiakids swell the strain.
Led by the Graces I, by sons of Lampon summoned o'er,
===Chinese===
收束节 1

那便是福星高照，美名清芬。
莫求与宙斯等同；只要这些至美的天赋
有一份落到你的份上，你便拥有了一切；
凡人的界限终究限定着凡人。
但弗拉基达斯啊，在地峡，有一份
不朽声名的双重酬报正为你贮备；在涅墨亚，为你们兄弟——
你与皮忒阿斯——备下了全能搏击的冠冕。啊，我的心
若无埃阿科斯（Aiakus）的子孙加入合唱，便再尝不到歌之狂喜。
我由美惠女神（Graces）引路，应兰蓬诸子之召而来，

===Original===
Strophe 2

To this, the city of fair governance, came. If she
To the clear path of deeds that Gods inspire
Hath turned her steps, grudge not the wine of minstrelsy,
Her valour's glory-hire.
Yea, for in days heroic her brave sons earned glory's crown,
And lyre and flute-notes manifold still peal out their renown
===Chinese===
正旋节 2

来到这座善理邦政的城。倘若她
已把脚步转向诸神默启的
清明之道，就请莫吝惜颂歌的醇酒——
那是她英武应得的荣耀雇价。
是啊，在英雄的年代，她的勇儿便挣得荣耀之冠，
竖琴与繁复的笛音至今仍把他们的英名轰然传扬

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

Through years past numbering. By Zeus Kronion's grace
A new theme Oineus' mighty sons have found
For bards inspired: Aetolians still with altar-blaze
Worship the world-renowned;
And chariot-speeding Iolaus still is Thebe's pride,
Of Argos Perseus, Leda's sons Eurotas' stream beside.
===Chinese===
反旋节 2

历经不可胜数的岁月。蒙克洛诺斯之子宙斯的恩典，
俄纽斯（Oineus）的英武儿郎们为受灵感的歌人
寻得了新的主题：埃托利亚（Aetolians）人至今仍以祭坛的烈焰
崇敬那举世闻名的英灵；
御车疾驰的伊俄拉俄斯仍是忒拜的骄傲，
阿尔戈斯有珀耳修斯（Perseus），勒达（Leda）的儿郎们则傍着欧罗塔斯河。

===Original===
Epode 2

And still Oenone worships the renown
Of Aiakus and his sons high-hearted, they
Who in stern battle sacked the Trojans' town
First, when with Heracles they faced the fray,
Then, with the sons of Atreus:—upward wing, O Muse, thy flight!
Tell who were they before whom Kyknus fell, and Hector died.
Who smote the dauntless chief who led the Aethiop hosts to fight,
Memnon the brazen-mailed? And who, Kaïkus' stream beside,
Met valiant Telephus, and with resistless spear did smite?
===Chinese===
收束节 2

而奥诺涅（Oenone）依然崇敬
埃阿科斯与他高贵儿郎们的英名——他们
曾在惨烈的战斗中攻掠特洛伊人的城池，
头一回随赫拉克勒斯迎面赴战，
再一回随阿特柔斯（Atreus）诸子：——缪斯啊，请振翅高飞！
告诉我，库克诺斯（Kyknus）跌倒在谁的面前，赫克托耳（Hector）死于谁手？
是谁击杀了那位统领埃塞俄比亚大军赴战的无畏统帅、
青铜铠甲的门农（Memnon）？又是谁在卡伊科斯（Kaïkus）河畔
迎上勇武的忒勒福斯（Telephus），以无可抵挡的枪锋将他刺伤？

===Original===
Strophe 3

Even they whose home my lips proclaim the glorious isle
Aegina!—that tower builded long ago
For heroism's feet to scale her stately pile.
My tongue's true-aiming bow
Hath many a shaft whose flight shall sing their praise: yea, Aias' state
Can witness how her shipmen's prowess saved from thraldom's fate
===Chinese===
正旋节 3

正是他们的家园——我的唇舌要传告那光荣的海岛：
埃吉那！——那是远古筑起的高塔，
供英雄伟业的健足攀登她巍峨的层楼。
我的舌头这张瞄得精准的弓，
有无数支箭将飞鸣着赞颂他们：是啊，埃阿斯的城邦
可以为证：她的水手的英武怎样从奴役的命运中救出了

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

Salamis in that ruin-tempest heaven-sent,
When slaughter's hailstorm did on myriads fall—
Yet hush, O lips of mine, the vaunt irreverent!
Is not Zeus Lord of all?
He ordereth this and that. These late-won honours gladly hail
Sweet song that hymns the victor's joy. Now, whoso hears the tale
===Chinese===
反旋节 3

萨拉米斯（Salamis）——在那场自天而降的毁灭风暴之中，
屠戮的冰雹当头砸落在万千人身上——
可住口吧，我的唇，休吐这不敬的夸词！
宙斯岂非万有之主？
他安排这，也安排那。让甜美的歌欣然礼赞这新近
赢得的荣耀，咏唱胜利者的欢欣。如今，凡听到这故事——

===Original===
Epode 3

Of this Kleonikus' house, e'en let him dare
The athlete-strife! Not dulled is yet the fame
Of their long toils! Nought for the cost they care:
No power hath this their fiery hopes to tame!
Yea, also Pytheas do I praise, who schooled unerringly
His brother's hands to deal the blows whereby Phylakidas bare
To earth the limbs of rivals—ah, a cunning fighter he!
Ho, take for him a crown, and bring the fleecy fillet fair!
With song fresh-pinioned speed him on his path of victory!
===Chinese===
收束节 3

这克勒俄尼科斯（Kleonikus）家系的故事——就让他放胆
投入力士的角逐吧！他们长年辛劳
挣得的名声尚未黯淡！花费几何，他们全不在意：
任何力量也驯不服他们火热的希望！
是啊，我也要赞美皮忒阿斯：他分毫不差地调教出
兄弟的双手，正是那一击，教弗拉基达斯
把对手的身躯砸倒在地——啊，好一个机巧的斗士！
喂，为他捧上冠冕，把那雪白的羊毛系带来！
以新翮初丰的歌声，送他疾飞在凯旋的路上！
### VI / 第六首

===Original===
For Phylakidas of Aegina, on his victory in the Pankration, BC 484 (?) or BC 480 (?).
===Chinese===
致埃吉那的弗拉基达斯，贺其全能搏击赛夺冠，公元前 484 年（?）或公元前 480 年（?）。

===Original===
Strophe 1

As they do in a banquet of men when the revelry runneth high,
So do we mingle a second bowl of the Song-queen's strain.
Unto Tampon's athlete-seed do we render honour thereby.
Our first was outpoured to thee, Zeus, in the day that saw us gain
The crown of all crowns at Nemea; the second this day pour we
To the Lord of the Isthmus and Nereus' fifty Maids of the Sea
For the House's youngest scion Phylakidas' victory.
Oh may we make ready a third for the Saviour Olympus' Lord!29
So may a libation of honey-sweet songs on Aegina be poured!
===Chinese===
正旋节 1

正如人们在宴席上酒酣兴浓时那样，
我们也斟满歌之女王颂调的第二杯。
以此向坦蓬（Tampon）的力士苗裔献上敬意。
我们的第一杯，宙斯啊，曾浇奠给你，在那一天——我们赢得
涅墨亚诸冠之冠的日子；这第二杯，今天我们浇奠
给地峡之主和涅柔斯（Nereus）的五十位海中女儿，
庆贺这家门最幼的子弟弗拉基达斯的胜利。
啊，愿我们还能为救主、奥林波斯之主备下第三杯！29
那样，蜜一般甘甜的歌之奠酒便可洒遍埃吉那！

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

For if one of the sons of men who exults in the cost and the toil
Attain to achievement that shall like a god-built tower stand,
And with Heaven's help plant the seed of renown in a fruitful soil,
God-honoured he casteth his anchor on Fortune's farthest strand.
Unto such desires to attain this son of Kleonymus prays
Or ever he cometh with death or with hoar hairs face to face.
And of Klotho enthroned on high this day I implore her grace,
Praying her and her sister Fates propitiously now to draw near
To the heavenward-ringing petition of him that I hold so dear.
===Chinese===
反旋节 1

因为世人中若有人乐于耗费钱财与辛劳，
终于建树起一项如神工高塔般屹立的功业，
又蒙上天的扶助，把声名之种播进沃土，
蒙神礼敬的他，便把锚抛在命运最远端的岸畔。
克勒俄尼摩斯的这位儿子所祈求的，正是这样的抱负——
趁他尚未与死亡或皓皓白发迎面相逢。
今天，我恳求高踞宝座的克洛托（Klotho）垂赐恩典，
祈愿她与她的命运姐妹们欣然就近，
来听我这至亲之人直冲霄汉的祈愿。

===Original===
Epode 1

And you, O Aiakus' sons, upon golden chariots mounted,
I deem it a sacred ordinance laid most clearly on me,
Whensoe'er I set foot on your isle, to shower on you praise; for uncounted
Highways of five-score feet stretch farther than eye can see
For your noble achievements: they pass to southward beyond Nile's fountains,
And away to the land lying north of where Boreas leaps from his mountains.
No city there is so uncouth of speech, but hath heard the story
Of the blest one, spouse of a Goddess, of hero Peleus' glory,
===Chinese===
收束节 1

而你们，埃阿科斯的儿郎们啊，端坐于黄金战车，
我深信有一道神圣的律令清清楚楚加在我身上：
每逢我踏上你们的海岛，便当把赞美如雨洒向你们；因为数不清的
百尺大道为你们的高贵功业铺展，远过目力所及：
它们向南伸过尼罗河的源头，
向北直抵玻瑞阿斯（Boreas）跃出山峦以外的那片国土。
天下没有哪座城邑言语这般粗陋，以致不曾听说过
那位有福者的故事——女神的夫婿、英雄珀琉斯（Peleus）的荣耀，

===Original===
Strophe 2

And hath heard of Aias Telamon's scion, and Aias' sire.
Him did Alkmena's son, to requite Laomedon's lie,
Lead with his warriors of Tiryns, an ally whose soul was afire
For the joy of the harness of battle, in galleys led him to Troy,
To the land of heroes' travail. So Pergamus-city was laid
In the dust by Heracles' might. Thereafter, with Telamon's aid,
The tribes of the Meropes slew he, against him in battle arrayed,
And the herdman huge as a mountain, Alkyoneus, whom he found
In Phlegra, and spared not his bowstring's thunder-clanging rebound.
===Chinese===
正旋节 2

也听说过埃阿斯——忒拉蒙（Telamon）的后裔——以及埃阿斯的父亲。
阿尔克墨涅之子为了清算拉俄墨冬（Laomedon）的欺谎，
率领提林斯（Tiryns）的武士，带着那位灵魂为
披甲作战之乐燃烧的盟友，乘战船把他带到特洛伊（Troy），
那英雄历尽辛劳的国土。就这样，帕伽摩斯（Pergamus）城
在赫拉克勒斯的威力下仆于尘埃。其后，他又得忒拉蒙之助，
诛灭了列阵相抗的墨罗普斯（Meropes）各部落，
还有那山岳般魁伟的牧人阿尔库俄纽斯（Alkyoneus）——他在
弗勒格拉（Phlegra）撞见他，弓弦雷霆般震响反弹，绝不容情。

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

But when Heracles came to bid to the voyaging Aiakus' son,
Him with his company feasting he found, and as there he stood
In the lion-skin, Telamon called on the son of Amphitryon
The first libation to pour of the cluster's nectar-blood;
And the chalice rough with the gold embossed with the word did he place
Wine-brimming in Heracles' hands. Thereupon did the hero raise
His hands, the invincible hands, in prayer to the firmament's space;
And he lifted his voice: "If ever, O Zeus Allfather, thou
Hast hearkened with willing soul unto prayer of mine, O now
===Chinese===
反旋节 2

而当赫拉克勒斯前去邀请埃阿科斯之子同赴远航，
他正与随从们宴饮；赫拉克勒斯身披
狮皮立在那里，忒拉蒙便请安菲特律翁之子
先行浇奠，把葡萄的甘美血汁洒祭于地；
他把那只錾金凸起、镌有铭文的酒盏
斟满了酒，放进赫拉克勒斯手中。于是这位英雄
向苍穹之上举起他无敌的双手祈祷；
他扬声说道："万父宙斯啊，倘若你
曾欣然垂听过我的祈求，那么此刻——

===Original===
Epode 2

"With heavenward-soaring prayers unto thee do I make my petition
To perfect in Eriboia's womb for the man at my side
A valiant son, who shall aye be my friend by Fate's decision,
One of thews invincibly stalwart, hard as the lion's hide
That at Nemea, first of my labours, I slew, this fell enfolding
My shoulders, and may his courage be worthy his frame's strong moulding!"
He spake, and a mighty eagle the God sent down from his heaven,
Monarch of birds; and with rapture thrilled for the omen given
===Chinese===
收束节 2

"我以直上云霄的祈祷向你陈请：
求你为身旁这人，在埃里波亚（Eriboia）的腹中
造就一个英勇的儿子——依命运的裁断，他永世是我的朋友——
愿他筋腱雄健、不可征服，坚韧如那狮子
的皮——那是我苦役的头一项，在涅墨亚杀它，剥皮
披上我的肩——愿他的胆魄配得上这雄伟身躯的铸型！"
他言毕，神明便自天庭降下一只巨鹰，
百鸟之王；为这所示的吉兆，众人狂喜震颤

===Original===
Strophe 3

Heracles lifted his voice, and he spake as speaketh a seer:
"Lo, thou shalt have the son thou desirest, Telamon;
And after the name of the bird that thou sawest but now appear,
So shalt thou name him, Aias, a world-famed mighty one,
In the battle-toils of thy people a warrior deadly strong."
So spake he, and sat him down. But for me it were all too long
Of all their achievements to tell. I came, O Queen of Song,
For Phylakidas, Pytheas, Euthymenes, the march to array
Of the triumph-procession, and brief, after Argive wont, be the lay.
===Chinese===
正旋节 3

赫拉克勒斯扬声说话，如先知一般：
"听着，忒拉蒙，你渴望的儿子必赐给你；
你就照你方才见到的那只鸟的名字，
为他取名埃阿斯——他将名震寰宇、力大无穷，
在你族人的战阵艰辛中做一名雄猛的战士。"
言毕他坐下。而他们所有的功业，要我一一道来
实在太长。歌之女王啊，我此来，
是要为弗拉基达斯、皮忒阿斯、欧提墨涅斯（Euthymenes）排布
凯旋的行列——颂歌便循阿尔戈斯的风尚，以简短为宜。

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

In Isthmian pankration victories three did they win by their might,
And from leaf-shadowed Nemea yet more triumphs, those glorious boys,
And their mother's brother. How fair a portion of song to the light
Did they bring! And with brightest dews of refreshing did they rejoice
The Clan of the Psalychidae; and now have they raised to renown
By their prowess the House of Themistius; yea, and in this good town
Do they dwell, whereon the Gods with loving eyes look down.
And, honouring Hesiod's words—"Whatsoever he findeth to do,"
That Lampon "doth with his might," and exhorteth his sons thereto.
===Chinese===
反旋节 3

凭着他们的膂力，他们在地峡全能搏击赛中三度夺魁，
又从绿叶荫覆的涅墨亚带回更多凯旋——那些光荣的少年，
还有他们母亲的兄弟。他们把多么美好的一份歌之题材
带到了天日之下！又以最清亮的醒神甘露，
畅快了普萨吕基达（Psalychidae）氏族的心；如今他们又以自身英武
把忒弥斯提俄斯（Themistius）的家门举向荣名；是啊，他们居住的这座
美好城邑，正有诸神以慈爱的眼目垂顾。
他们敬奉赫西俄德（Hesiod）的话——"凡你手所当作的事，"
兰蓬便"尽力去作"，并以此勉励他的儿子们。

===Original===
Epode 3

So he brings to his city glory, the weal of the whole state serving.
He is loved for his kindness to strangers: the golden mean alway
In purpose, the golden mean in action he follows unswerving.
His tongue is at one with his thoughts. Amid athletes he is, thou canst say,
As the Naxian stone that in grinding of bronze all other excelleth.
I will give him to drink of Dirke's taintless spring that upwelleth
By the stately-rampired gates of the city of Kadmus, whose waters
Were caused to leap to the light by Memory's deep-zoned daughters.
===Chinese===
收束节 3

他就这样为城邦赢来荣耀，以全邦的福祉为务。
他因厚待宾客而见爱：无论存心
还是行事，他始终如一地持守那黄金般的中道。
他的舌头与心思如一。在力士之中，你尽可说，
他好比纳克索斯（Naxos）的磨石，磨砺青铜时压倒群伦。
我要请他饮狄尔刻那无垢的清泉——它涌流在
卡德摩斯之城雄堞环卫的城门旁；那泉水
是"记忆"束着深深腰带的女儿们引它跃向光明。

### VII / 第七首

===Original===
For Strepsiades of Thebes, on his victory in the Pankration, BC 456 (?). The victor's uncle had recently been killed in battle.
===Chinese===
致忒拜的斯特瑞普西阿德斯（Strepsiades），贺其全能搏击赛夺冠，公元前 456 年（?）。获胜者的叔父新近战死沙场。

===Original===
Strophe 1

In which of the old-time glories that made thy land renowned
Hath thy spirit, O happy Thebe, delighted most of all?
When thou sawest the birth of the God of the tresses that toss unbound,
Dionysus, enthroned by Demeter to whom clashed cymbals call?
Or when thou didst welcome the chief of the Gods at the midnight hour,
What time he descended to earth in a golden-snowing shower,
===Chinese===
正旋节 1

在那些使你的国土扬名的古老荣光之中，
你的心灵，幸福的忒拜啊，最以哪一桩为乐？
是你亲见那披散飞扬的发缕之神诞生之时——
狄俄尼索斯（Dionysus），在铙钹铿鸣呼唤的得墨忒耳身旁登座？
还是你在午夜时分迎接诸神之王，
那时他化作金色的雪雨纷纷，降落到人间，

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

When he stood at Amphitryon's portal, and went in unto the bride
Of Amphitryon, whence sprang god-begotten Heracles?
Was it when Teiresias' counsels inspired were thy joy and thy pride?
Was it when thou didst see Iolaus' chariot-masteries,
Or the Sown Men's tireless spears? Or when from thy fierce war-shout
Thou sentest Adrastus fleeing, bereft of the battle-rout
===Chinese===
反旋节 1

他立在安菲特律翁的门前，走进安菲特律翁
新娘的房中，于是诞下神生的赫拉克勒斯？
是提瑞西阿斯（Teiresias）受灵感的谏言为你所乐、为你所傲之时？
是你亲见伊俄拉俄斯御车的绝技，
或那播种武士们不知疲倦的长枪之时？还是当你厉声的战吼
逐得阿德拉斯托斯亡命奔逃、抛下了成军的部众——

===Original===
Epode 1

Of his countless comrades, back unto Argos the war-steed land?
Or when thou didst set the feet of the Dorian Spartans again
Firm in the ancient home, and when by a warrior-band,
Even thy sons of the Aegeid House, was Amyklae ta'en
Because they obeyed the Pythian oracle's command?
But alas! it sleepeth, the olden glory,
And mortals forget the heroic story,
===Chinese===
收束节 1

——无数的同袍——逃回那战马之邦阿尔戈斯？
还是你重新安顿多里安斯巴达人的双足，
使他们稳立于古老的家园；抑或那一队武士——
正是你埃阿吉德（Aegeid）家系的儿郎——攻取了阿米克莱（Amyklae），
只因他们遵从了皮托神谕的号令？
然而唉！那古老的荣光沉睡了，
世人也把英雄的故事遗忘，

===Original===
Strophe 2

Save only that which attains unto poesy's perfect flower
By reason that it hath been wedded to far-ringing streams of song.
For Strepsiades then lead forth the procession in this glad hour
With strains sweet-rippling. He brings the pankratian meed of the strong
From Isthmus. In strength is he wondrous, and goodly withal to behold;
Nor his stature is shamed by his valour, his spirit aweless-bold.
===Chinese===
正旋节 2

唯有凭仗与流传悠远的歌之长河缔结良缘、
从而绽出诗艺完美之花的，才免于湮没。
那么，就在这欢欣的时辰，以甜漾的歌流
引领斯特瑞普西阿德斯的队伍前行吧。他从地峡
带回力士全能搏击的酬报。他神力惊人，仪表亦堂堂可观；
他的胆魄无畏无惧，身量也毫不有愧于那份勇武。

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

Glows on him a splendour breathed by the flower-tressed Muses' breath.
A share in his crown to his namesake mother's brother he gave,
For whom Ares the brazen-bucklered mingled the wine of death.
Yet a recompense of renown is laid up in store for the brave;
For let him be assured—whosoe'er, overgloomed by the cloud of war,
Beats back the hailstorm of blood from his dear land's heart afar,
===Chinese===
反旋节 2

花鬓垂云的缪斯呼出的光华，正辉映在他身上。
他把冠冕中的一份分给与他同名的舅父——
那执青铜圆盾的阿瑞斯（Ares）已为他调好死亡之酒的人。
然而声名的报偿早已为勇士储藏；
请他坚信——无论何人，纵使被战争的阴云笼罩，
仍把血雨的冰雹从亲爱乡土的心脏边击回，

===Original===
Epode 2

By hurling death through the ranks of the host of his fatherland's foe—
Be assured that he maketh his nation's glory to shine more bright,
Yea, whether he live, or whether the hero in death lie low.
But thou, O scion of Diodotus, in that last fight
With strong Meleager didst vie—yea, as his did thy battle-fire glow!—
And with Hector and Amphiaraus vying
Didst breathe out youth's fair bloom in thy dying
===Chinese===
收束节 2

把死亡掷进祖国敌军那大军的长阵——
请坚信：他必使邦国的荣耀更加灿烂，
无论他生还，抑或英雄长眠于沙场。
而你，狄奥多托斯（Diodotus）的子弟啊，在那最后一战，
你与雄强的墨勒阿革罗斯（Meleager）争衡——是啊，你的战火燃得同他一样旺！——
你又与赫克托耳、安菲阿拉俄斯（Amphiaraus）竞比，
在临终之际吐尽了青春绚烂的芳华

===Original===
Strophe 3

In the press of the battle, the forefront of fight, where of warriors our chief
Bare up the weight of the struggle of war in hope's despair.
Ah me! at the woeful tidings I suffered unspeakable grief!
By the Earth-enfolder's grace now calm after storm shines fair.
With garlands enwreathing my locks will I sing this victory.
O may not the triumph be marred by the high Gods' jealousy,
===Chinese===
正旋节 3

在鏖战的挤迫中、在交锋的最前列，我们的将士之首
曾在希望断绝之际独力担起战争的重负。
哀哉！噩耗传来，我悲恸难可言表！
如今蒙大地环拥者的恩典，风暴过后晴空朗朗。
我要将花冠缠上鬓发，歌唱这一场胜利。
啊，愿高天诸神的嫉恨莫来玷损这凯旋，

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

As onward I follow to taste the sweetness of this my day,
And peacefully journey to eld and the bourne that Fate doth ordain
For my life. For we all must die: alike are we passing away,
Though our fortune be diverse. How far soever one's gaze may strain,
Too frail is man to attain to the heaven brazen-floored.
Even so did wingèd Pegasus fling his earthly lord,
===Chinese===
反旋节 3

当我一路前行，细品我今日的甘美，
又安然走向暮年，走向命运为我的生命
预定的彼岸。因为我们人人必死：际遇纵然万殊，
我们同样在消逝。任凭目光极望多远，
人也太过孱弱，登不上那青铜铺底的高天。
正是如此，生翼的珀伽索斯（Pegasus）把它的凡间骑手掀翻在地，

===Original===
Epode 3

When Bellerophon fain would have winged his flight to the mansions on high,
And have entered the glorious conclave of Gods with Zeus throned there.
Bitter the end is of pleasure attained unlawfully.
But to us, O Loxias, thou with thy glory of golden hair
Ever blooming in youth, do thou with a gift of thy grace draw nigh,
From Pytho's contests on us bestowing
A garland of bright flowers lovely-blowing.
===Chinese===
收束节 3

那时柏勒罗丰（Bellerophon）妄图振翅直上高天的宫阙，
跻身宙斯御座所在的诸神辉煌的圣会。
以非分之径攫取的欢愉，终局必是苦涩。
但对我们，洛克西阿斯（Loxias）啊，你这金发的荣光
在青春中常开不败的神明，请携恩泽的馈赠垂临，
把皮托赛会上那顶
明花盛放的花冠赐予我们。
### VIII / 第八首

===Original===
For Kleandros of Aegina, on his victory in the boys' Pankration, BC 478 (?). It was shortly after the battle of Plataea: the poet, while thankful for the liberation of Greece, is filled with sorrow for the traitorous part which his native Thebes has played in that war.
===Chinese===
致埃吉那的克勒安德罗斯（Kleandros），贺其少年组全能搏击赛夺冠，公元前 478 年（?）。时值普拉提亚（Plataea）战役后不久：诗人一面感念希腊（Hellas）重获自由，一面为故乡忒拜在那场战争中的附敌行径满怀悲怆。

===Original===
Strophe 1

For Kleandros and his comrades, O ye youths, let some of you
Go and stand before his father Telesarchus' gleaming door,
And wake the chant, the recompense for toils, his glorious due
For the crowns that from the Isthmus and from Nemea he bore.
What though I be stricken-hearted, to their praying have I hearkened,
And on the golden Muse I call. From night of woes that darkened
Around us are we rescued, and we may not brook bereaving
Of triumph-crowns, nor over cureless evils linger grieving.
But let us cheer the people with the sweetness of our song,
Though the pain have scarce departed; for the stone that hung so long
Above our heads—a very stone of Tantalus—at last
A God hath turned aside: the peril now is overpast,
===Chinese===
正旋节 1

青年们啊，为着克勒安德罗斯和他的伙伴们，请你们中几位
去到他父亲忒勒萨尔科斯（Telesarchus）亮堂的家门前站定，
唱醒那支颂歌——辛劳的酬报，是他应得的荣光：
为着他从地峡与涅墨亚捧回的那几顶冠冕。
我虽心头带伤，仍侧耳听了他们的祈请，
呼唤黄金的缪斯。那曾在四围
阴暗的苦难之夜，我们已得救拔；凯旋的冠冕不容褫夺，
对无可疗救的灾殃，也不该抱憾流连。
且让我们以歌的甘美鼓舞民众，
虽然创痛犹未消散；那块久久
悬在我们头顶的巨石——活脱是坦塔罗斯（Tantalus）之石——终于
被一位神明拨开：险难如今已成过去，

===Original===
Strophe 2

That intolerable curse unto Hellas! But the night
Of terror hath departed, and the crushing load of care
Is lifted. Yet 'tis better evermore to keep in sight
The perils that be waiting in the path whereon we fare.
For Time is dogging mortals' steps, with treacherous feet on-stealing,
And tangling all the ways of life. Yet even here is healing
For such as have but freedom. Let us still to hope be clinging!
In Thebe seven-gated nursed, the Graces' flowers of singing
I needs must give Aegina; for Asopus' daughters twain
Found favour in the sight of Zeus who over all doth reign.
And one of these by lovely-flowered Dirke's fair demesne
He caused to dwell, to be the chariot-loving city's queen.
===Chinese===
正旋节 2

——那场落在希腊身上不堪忍受的灾殃！恐怖的
长夜已经退去，压得人喘不过气的忧担
也已卸下。然而永远要看清
在我们行进的路上潜伏的种种危难。
因为时间正尾随着凡人的脚步，以欺诈的步履悄然逼近，
把人生条条道路缠得错乱。但即使在这里，
只要拥有自由，便有医治。让我们仍紧抱着希望！
我这朵在七门忒拜哺育、由美惠女神亲手栽种的歌之花，
不能不献给埃吉那；因为阿索波斯（Asopus）的两位女儿
都蒙那统治万有的宙斯垂青。
其中一位，他让她住在花开可爱的狄尔刻的秀美领地，
做那酷爱战车之城的王后。

===Original===
Strophe 3

But thee unto Oenopia's isle he bore, and couched with thee.
And there unto the Thunder-crashing Father didst thou bear
A godlike scion, Aiakus, of men most righteous he;
Yea, even to the Gods in Heaven contention's arbiter
Was he. His hero-sons and children's children war-delighting
With peerless valour met the clanging bronze of warriors fighting;
And self-controlled they ruled their spirit, even as wisdom taught them.
Now when the Gods in council met, of all this they bethought them,
When Zeus with glory-girt Poseidon in contention strove
For Thetis' hand; for either God was captive of her love,
And fain would win the fairest; but impossible it was
That Heaven's eternal counsels should bring such a thing to pass
===Chinese===
正旋节 3

而你，他把你携往埃诺庇亚（Oenopia）的海岛，与你同衾共枕。
在那里，你为那雷霆轰鸣的父亲
生下一位神明般的子嗣——埃阿科斯，世人至正至直的一位；
是啊，甚至天上诸神起了争执，
也由他仲裁。他的英雄儿孙辈酷嗜战争，
以无双的英勇迎战厮杀中铿锵相击的青铜；
而他们克己自持、驾驭心神，一如智慧所教。
后来诸神共聚议事，这一切便被想起——
那时宙斯与荣耀环身的波塞冬争执不下，
都要娶忒提斯（Thetis）为妻；因为两位神明都成了她爱宠的俘虏，
都想赢得这至美的女神；然而上天永恒的定议
断不容许这样的事成就，

===Original===
Strophe 4

Whence once they heard the oracle that spake them Fate's decree.
For Themis wise in counsel in the Gods' assembly told
How that Destiny ordained it that the Lady of the Sea
Should bear a son more mighty than his father, who should hold
In his hand another sceptre more resistless than the levin
Or the trident, were she wedded with any lord of Heaven:—
"Cease to contend! In marriage to a mortal be she given.
Like Ares' hands her son's shall be where battle's strife is striven,
His speed of foot as lightning-flash;—but she shall see him die
In war! I give my counsel that this honour from on high
To Peleus son of Aiakus be granted. All attest
That dweller in Iolkos' plain of mortals holiest.
===Chinese===
正旋节 4

他们由此听见了宣示命运裁断的神谕。
因为足智多谋的忒弥斯（Themis）曾在诸神的集会上宣告：
命运已然注定：这位海中贵女将生一个
强过父亲的儿子；她若嫁与天上的任何主宰，
那儿子手中将握另一支权杖，比雷霆
或三叉戟更难抵挡：——
"别再相争！把她嫁给一个凡人吧。
在战斗厮杀之处，她儿子的双手将如阿瑞斯的手，
他的脚快如掣电；——但她将亲眼见他
死于战争！我献上我的计议：把这份自高天而降的荣耀
赐给埃阿科斯之子珀琉斯。众口一同作证：
那住在伊奥尔科斯（Iolkos）平原上的人，是凡人中最圣洁的。

===Original===
Strophe 5

"Then let our message straight go forth to Cheiron's cave divine,
Nor once again let Nereus' daughter set the leaves of strife
In hands of Gods; but when the midmonth orbèd moonbeams shine
At eventide, then let her loose her maiden zone, the wife
Most lovely of a hero." So the Goddess spake, preventing
The ruin-day of Kronos' sons. And they in one consenting
Bent their immortal brows. Nor did her counsel's fruitage wither;
For told it is that these two Kings in friendship came together
To Thetis' bridal. And the lips of bards have published far
To them that saw it not the mighty prowess in the war
Of young Achilles, how he poured a dusky-crimson stain,
The life-blood of king Telephus, on Mysia's vine-clad plain,
===Chinese===
正旋节 5

"那就让我们的谕令径直送往喀戎（Cheiron）神圣的洞府，
不许涅柔斯的女儿再把纷争的叶子
放进诸神手中；待月中望日的圆月在
黄昏发光，便让她松开少女的腰带，去做
一位英雄最美的新娘。"女神如此说，抢在
克洛诺斯诸子的毁灭之日以前。诸神异口同声，
俯下不死的眉额。她的计议也并未结出枯萎的果实；
据传这两位君王情谊和睦，一同
来赴忒提斯的婚筵。而歌人的唇舌早已向
未能亲见的人们广为传布：年少阿喀琉斯（Achilles）在战阵中
是何等英武——他泼洒下一道暗红的血渍，
忒勒福斯王的生命之血——在密西亚（Mysia）藤蔓覆蔽的平原上，

===Original===
Strophe 6

And bridge-like paved the Atreids' safe return across the sea,
And rescued Helen; for he hewed asunder with his spear
Troy's sinews, them who strove to stay the slaughter-work that he
Wrought in that battle-harried plain, yea, stayed the proud career
Of Memnon's might, of Hector and the chiefs in strength excelling
Of Troy, to whom Achilles showed to Queen Persephone's dwelling
The path—the Aiakids' champion he!—and glory so was bringing
Unto Aegina and his race. Yea, lips immortal singing
Wailed o'er him dead, when Helicon's Maids, a many-voicèd choir,
Stood by his death-rites, pouring forth their dirge around the pyre.
Ay, so the Immortals willed that heroism, even in death,
Should be a theme for Goddesses to hymn with praising breath.
===Chinese===
正旋节 6

又如桥梁般铺平了阿特柔斯之子们渡海平安的归路，
救回了海伦（Helen）；他用长枪斩断
特洛伊的筋腱——那些人竭力想拦阻他在那片
惨遭兵燹的平原上完成的屠戮伟业，也想遏止
门农伟力的骄横进逼，遏止赫克托耳与特洛伊
众多膂力超群的酋豪——而阿喀琉斯给他们指明了
通往珀耳塞福涅（Persephone）女王宫邸的
路——他是埃阿科斯一族的魁首！——就这样把荣耀
带给了埃吉那和他的家族。是啊，不朽的唇舌为他之死哀恸悲鸣，
当赫利孔的缪斯——那众声齐鸣的歌队——在他的葬礼上侍立，
绕着柴堆涌出挽歌悲声。是啊，不死的诸神正愿如此：
让英雄的豪气纵在身后，也成为女神们以赞美之息咏唱的题目。

===Original===
Strophe 7

Yea, to this day that law of honour holds: the Muses' car
Speeds on to sound his glory forth who won the gauntlet-fight
In Isthmus' glade, even Nikokles.30 Oh, peal his praise afar
Who won the Dorian parsley-crown, who vanquished by his might,
He too,31 all rivals, hurled them back with leap as of a lion.
Nor him dishonoureth now his father's noble brother's scion.
Twine then, ye comrades of the victor, twine the tender greenness
Of myrtle for his brows! Alkathous' contest hailed his keenness
Of courage with fair fortune. Yea, with welcoming acclaim
The sons of Epidaurus met him. Meet it is the fame
Of such be sung by good men; for he hid not from the light,
As in oblivion's pit, the splendour of his youthful might.
===Chinese===
正旋节 7

是啊，直到今日那条荣典的法则仍然有效：缪斯的彩车
飞驰向前，为那在地峡林间空场赢得拳斗的
尼科克勒斯（Nikokles）传扬荣光。30 啊，让他的赞美远播四方——
他赢得了多里安的欧芹冠冕，以膂力克敌制胜，
他也将一切对手击退，如雄狮腾跃。31
如今他父亲高贵兄弟的后裔也未曾使他蒙羞。
那么，胜利者的伙伴们，缠起那柔嫩的青色，
为他的眉额缠上桃金娘吧！阿尔卡托斯（Alkathous）的赛会以美好的时运
迎接他昂扬的斗志。是啊，以欢迎的欢呼，
埃皮达鲁斯（Epidaurus）的儿郎们迎迓了他。这样的人的名声，
正该由善者咏唱；因为他不曾把自己的青春伟力
像埋进遗忘的深坑那样，藏匿于光明之外。

## The Nemean Odes / 尼米亚颂

### I / 一

===Original===
For Chromius of Etna, who claimed descent from Heracles, on his victory in the chariot-race, BC 476 (?).
===Chinese===
献给埃特纳的克罗弥俄斯（Chromius）——他自称是赫拉克勒斯的后裔——庆其战车赛获胜，公元前 476 年（?）。

===Original===
Strophe 1

O breathing-place of Alpheus panting in chase of the Nymph Arethusa, O child
Of Syracuse world-renowned, Ortygia, couch of the Huntress-queen of the wild,
O sister of Delos, the chant sweet-ringing is speeding from thee to proclaim with singing
The mighty glory of tempest-footed horses, by Zeus’ grace, Etna’s lord;
For the chariot of Chromius and Nemea stir me to yoke to her victory song’s reward.
===Chinese===
首节 1（Strophe 1）

哦，阿尔甫斯河喘息着追逐宁芙阿瑞图萨，你是他吐纳安歇的水乡，哦
举世闻名的叙拉古之女，奥尔提吉亚，荒野猎后的卧榻，
哦，得洛斯的姊妹，这清音甜美的颂歌正从你身边疾驰，要以歌唱昭告
疾风之足的骏马的赫赫荣光——托宙斯的恩典——埃特纳之主的荣光；
因为克罗弥俄斯的战车与尼米亚催动我，去为那胜利赛歌的酬报驾轭。

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

Lo, how the Song’s foundations are laid in homage of Gods, and in praise of the might
Of the victor’s godlike prowess! By fortune fair is he lifted to glory’s height.
Even the Song-queens joy in recording contests strenuous, garland-awarding.
Ho, scatter achievement’s splendour-seed o’er the isle Lord Zeus to Persephone gave
To be Queen of the land fruit-wealthy, and bowed the locks that o’er brows Olympian wave
===Chinese===
次节 1（Antistrophe 1）

看哪，歌的根基如何奠定——敬奉众神，礼赞那胜利者
神明般勇武的伟力！凭美好的命运，他被举上荣耀的高峰。
连歌队的女王们也乐于记述那些激烈的赛会、颁发花冠的角逐。
嗬，把功业的辉煌种子撒遍这座海岛——主神宙斯把它赐予珀耳塞福涅，
让她做这丰饶国土的女王；他俯首垂下了那在奥林波斯额前飘拂的鬈发，

===Original===
Epode 1

For his pledge to exalt her crowns of wealthy cities, this Sicily harvest-teeming;
And a people Kronion bestowed on her, wooers of war in bronze-hammered harness gleaming,
A folk of the spear and the steed, to be wedded full oft to Olympia’s olive-leaf golden:—
Lo, I have lighted on theme after theme, never falsely, but aye by the truth have I holden.
===Chinese===
末节 1（Epode 1）

作为他的誓约：要尊崇她那诸富庶城邦的花冠——这五谷丰登的西西里；
克洛诺斯之子又赐她一族人民，铜甲灿然、热恋战争的人，
一个持枪纵马的种族，将屡屡与奥林匹亚金橄榄叶缔结良缘：——
看哪，我连连接获题材，从无虚言，始终持守着真实。

===Original===
Strophe 2

Sweet are the strains that I sing as I stand at the doors of a hero who loveth the guest;
And there is arrayed a banquet meet for a bard in the halls whither oft have pressed
Strangers from far-off shores who departed:—O yea, he hath won for him friends true-hearted
By whom slander is quenched, as smouldering fire by water. Diverse be men in skill,
But in straight paths ever ’tis meet to walk, and to fight life’s battle as Nature shall will.
===Chinese===
首节 2

甘美的是我的歌——当我站在一位敬爱宾客的英雄门前吟唱；
他的厅堂里为诗人摆开了合宜的盛宴，那里常有远方的来客
慕名而至又欣然离去：——哦真的，他已为自己赢得真挚的朋友，
凭借他们，谗言如闷燃之火被水浇熄。人的才能各有不同，
但人行正道方为合宜，且要顺应天性，打好人生的这一仗。

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

Bodily strength in action worketh, but wisdom of soul in counsel, for one
In whom is inborn the skill to foresee the future. Agesidamus’ son,
To thee, life through, by the grace of Heaven, have strength and wisdom alike been given.
I love not to hoard in mine halls vast wealth, but to taste life’s pleasures and share life’s wine,
For my good name’s sake, with friends; for the hopes of toil-tried men I account as mine.
===Chinese===
次节 2

肉体的力量见之于行动，灵魂的智慧见之于谋略——对那
天生具备预见未来之才的人而言。阿革西达摩斯之子啊，
终你一生，上天的恩典把力量与智慧一并赐给了你。
我不爱在厅堂里囤积巨富，只愿品味人生的欢愉、共饮生命的醇酒，
为我美名的缘故，与朋友们同享；因为历尽辛劳者的希望，我也认作自己的希望。

===Original===
Epode 2

For me, my spirit is willing thrall to the fascination of Heracles’ glory;
Mid the heights of achievement whereunto he soared I love to recall that old-time story,
How, soon as the son of Zeus came forth to the light of day with his twin-born brother,
When he leapt to the splendour of sunlight-glow from the travail-tormented womb of his mother,
===Chinese===
末节 2

至于我，我的心灵甘愿受缚于赫拉克勒斯荣耀的魔力；
在他翱翔而至的功业高峰之中，我乐于追忆那古老的故事——
宙斯之子如何与他的孪生兄弟一同初见天日，
如何从母亲受难煎熬的腹中一跃而起，跃入灿灿的日光，

===Original===
Strophe 3

Then Hera the gold-enthroned marked well where the babe mid his saffron swaddlings lay;
And the Queen of the Gods, with anger stung, two serpents against him sent straightway.
Into the chamber, when opened its portal, they slid, those servants of hate immortal,
Ravening-eager to coil their swiftly-darting jaws round the children twain.
But Heracles straightway uplifted his head, and was first to essay the battle-strain,
===Chinese===
首节 3

那时，金座的赫拉看得分明，那婴儿正裹在藏红花的襁褓里安眠；
众神的王后怒火中烧，立刻派出两条巨蟒扑向他的摇篮。
门扉一开，它们便溜进内室，那两个不朽仇恨的仆从，
饿鹰般急切，要把飞窜的毒颚缠上这对孪生的孩童。
赫拉克勒斯却昂然抬头，第一个迎向这场搏战，

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

And of either serpent he gripped the throat in the hands wherefrom escape there was none,
Till the breath of life from their monstrous frames was breathed as the feet of time stole on.
But the arrow of horror soul-overpowering smote the maids round the bed of Alkmena cowering.
Yea, even she from her couch of the night had leapt of her tunic disarrayed,
And with weak woman-hands to beat the monsters’ tyrannous onslaught back she essayed.
===Chinese===
次节 3

他双手各攥住一条蛇的咽喉，任凭它们再无处逃遁，
直到时光的脚步悄然移过，两条巨怪才吐尽了性命的气息。
而恐怖之箭以其摄魂之力，射得阿尔克墨涅床前的侍女们瑟瑟蜷伏。
连她也从夜里的卧榻一跃而起，衣衫不整，
用纤弱的女人的双手，奋力抗击怪物们的凶暴进袭。

===Original===
Epode 3

And swiftly a throng of the chiefs Kadmeian came hurrying thither in bronze-mail clashing,
And thither the father Amphitryon hasted, his falchion bared from the sheath outflashing,
Smitten with keen-stabbing anguish: for each man’s grief on his own soul heavily presseth;
But soon disburdened of grief is the heart that nought but another’s affliction distresseth.
===Chinese===
末节 3

一队卡德摩斯家的首领迅速赶来，铜甲铿锵，
父亲安菲特律翁也疾奔而至，弯刀出鞘，寒光闪耀，
锥心的剧痛煎熬着他：因为各人的悲愁总沉沉压着自己的灵魂；
而只为他人苦难所扰的心，却很快能卸下悲怀。

===Original===
Strophe 4

And there with his soul in a turmoil of wonder and rapture past all bearing he stood
Beholding the tokens of giant strength and the child’s unearthly-aweless mood:
For to falsehood the tale of the messengers’ telling by the Gods had been turned. Then one near-dwelling
He summoned, Teiresias ever-unerring seer, the prophet of Zeus most high;
And to him and to all his host the child’s life-fortune did that seer prophesy.
===Chinese===
首节 4

他伫立在那里，心中惊疑与狂喜翻腾，难以自持，
注视着这婴儿巨人力气的征象、那超凡无畏的神情：
因为众神已使报信人所传的凶信化为虚妄。于是他召来一位近旁居住的
先知——那从不失误的忒瑞西阿斯，至高的宙斯的预言者；
先知便为他，也为满堂众人，预言了这孩子一生的命运。

===Original===
Antistrophe 4

For he told how many justice-defying monsters on land and sea he should slay,
And should give unto death a man most hateful who walked in malice’s crooked way;
Yea also and when the strife should be striven on Phlegra’s plain of the Dwellers in Heaven
Against the earth-spawned Giants arrayed, then ’neath his arrows’ rushing rain
Should the flame-bright hair of the monster-brood be fouled in death with dust of the plain.
===Chinese===
次节 4

先知讲道：他将诛灭多少蔑视正义的怪物，在陆上与海中，
还要把那心怀歹毒、行于邪路的最可憎之人送上死路；
还讲到，当天上众神在佛勒格拉平原与大地所生的
巨人军团开战之时，在他的飞箭如骤雨倾泻之下，
那怪物族类火亮的鬈发将在死亡里染上平原的尘土。

===Original===
Epode 4

But himself, at rest from his mighty toils, should thereafter inherit through days unending
Peace ever-during, for sufferings past a recompense all earth-joys transcending,
In the mansions of bliss wherein, united to Hebe blooming in youth eternal,
With Zeus Kronion he sits at the feast in the deep content of a home supernal.
===Chinese===
末节 4

而他自己，在伟大的劳役止息之后，将在此后无尽的岁月里承继
永恒的安宁，作为已历苦难的报偿，超越尘世一切的欢愉，
在那极乐的宫邸之中，他与青春永驻的赫柏结为连理，
同克洛诺斯之子宙斯安坐筵席，在天上的家园里享深深的满足。
### II / 二

===Original===
For Timodemus of Acharnae in Attica, who had been trained in Salamis, on his victory in the Pancration, BC 485 (?).
===Chinese===
献给阿提卡阿卡尔奈的提摩得摩斯（Timodemus）——他曾在萨拉米斯受训——庆其潘克拉提亚（Pancration，混合格斗）获胜，公元前 485 年（?）。

===Original===
Strophe 1

As the sons of Homer, the singers of deftly-woven lays,
Ever begin their chants with a prelude in Zeus’s praise,
So in the Grove whose glory is chanted in every nation
This hero-athlete hath laid his achievements’ first foundation
Where in Nemean Zeus’s name are bestowed the victor’s bays.
===Chinese===
首节 1

正如荷马的子孙，那编织精巧歌谣的歌人，
总以颂扬宙斯的序曲开启他们的吟唱，
这位英雄运动员也在这片圣林之中——它的荣光传诵于万邦——
为他的功业奠下最初的基石，
在尼米亚，人们以宙斯之名授予胜者的桂冠。

===Original===
Strophe 2

And if She, who unswerving hath guided his feet, even Destiny,
On the path by his forefathers trodden, hath given this man to be
A glory to mighty Athens, he surely is fated victorious,
This son of Timonous, often to pluck the flower most glorious
Of the Isthmian Games, and at Pytho to win the victory;
===Chinese===
首节 2

如果那位目不斜视引导他脚步的神明——命运，
已在这条他祖先踏过的路上，注定此人成为
强大的雅典的荣耀，那么他命定胜利无疑——
提摩努斯之子啊，他将一再摘取地峡赛会
至为辉煌的花朵，并在皮托赢得胜利；

===Original===
Strophe 3

For ’tis meet that Orion’s rising should follow exceeding nigh
To the Pleiad Maids of the Mountain. Few can with Salamis vie
As a nurse of warriors mighty: yea. Hector in Troy’s war-leaguer
Heard Aias’ challenge; and thee shall thy prowess contest-eager
In the fivefold grapple, O Timodemus, glorify.
===Chinese===
首节 3

因为俄里翁的升起理应紧紧相随
山中普勒阿得斯姊妹的脚步。能同萨拉米斯媲美、
养育雄壮武士者寥寥无几：是啊，赫克托耳在特洛伊的战围中
也曾领教埃阿斯的挑战；而你，提摩得摩斯，
你那渴求争胜的勇武，将在五项竞技中为你增辉。

===Original===
Strophe 4

Acharnae, as tell old legends, for hero-sons is renowned;
And in all that pertaineth to contests pre-eminent still hath been found
This Timodemus’ House: in Parnassus imperial-seated
Have they won four victory-wreaths, strong champions aye undefeated.
Yea, also in royal Pelops’ mountain-folds were they crowned
===Chinese===
首节 4

阿卡尔奈，如古老的传说所讲，以英雄之子闻名；
而凡属卓越赛会的一切，至今都可见
提摩得摩斯家族的身影：在王座般高踞的帕尔纳索斯，
他们已赢得四顶胜利的花冠，刚强的冠军从未败北。
是啊，在王者珀罗普斯的山间谷地他们也曾加冕，

===Original===
Strophe 5

Eight times by the sons of Corinth; in Nemea withal did they gain
Seven triumph-wreaths; and at home, where Olympian Zeus’s fane
Looks down on the contest, garlands whose number passeth the telling.
Let Timodemus, O citizens, hear your acclaim upswelling
Hailing his home-return! Now upraise ye the sweet-ringing strain.
===Chinese===
首节 5

八次由科林斯人之手；在尼米亚他们同样赢得
七顶凯旋的花冠；而在家乡，奥林波斯的宙斯神庙
俯瞰赛场之处，花冠之多已难数计。
公民们，让提摩得摩斯听见你们的欢呼高昂，
迎接他的凯旋归来！现在，扬起这清音悠扬的歌行。

### III / 三

===Original===
For Aristokleides of Aegina, on his victory in the Pancration, BC 475 (?), some years before; written for an anniversary of the victory.
===Chinese===
献给埃伊纳的阿里斯托克莱德斯（Aristokleides），庆其潘克拉提亚获胜，公元前 475 年（?）或更早数年；此歌为胜利周年纪念而作。

===Original===
Strophe 1

O queenly Muse, our mother, hitherward come, I pray,
When the holy Moon brings round the Nemean festal day,
To Aegina the guest-thronged Dorian isle. Where the ripples are sliding
Of Asopian waves, young craftsmen of songs honey-savoured, abiding
Thy coming, are longing to hear thy voice’s great song-burden!
===Chinese===
首节 1

哦，王后般的缪斯，我们的母亲，恳请你翩然降临，
当圣洁的月亮把尼米亚的节期带到眼前，
降临埃伊纳——宾客如云的多利安海岛。在阿索波斯
波浪的涟漪滑动之处，年轻的歌之巧匠们正守候
你的来临，渴望听见你嗓音中宏大的歌之重负！

===Original===
Sooth, diverse deeds ever thirst for many a diverse guerdon,
But victory in these Games above all things loveth Song
Meetest companion of crowns and of triumphs achieved by the strong.
===Chinese===
真的，各样的功业总渴求各样不同的酬报，
而这些赛会中的胜利超乎一切，最爱歌唱——
它是强者赢得的花冠与凯旋最相称的伴侣。

===Original===
Antistrophe 1

O Muse, unto me full measure of inspiration accord,
And do thou, his daughter, upraise to the cloud-thronged heaven’s Lord
A noble hymn: I will blend it—its strains as in spousals allying
With the lyre and the voices of singers. Aegina’s glorifying
===Chinese===
次节 1

哦，缪斯，请赐我满溢的灵感，
而你，他的女儿，向那云霭簇拥的天穹之主
献上一支高贵的颂歌：我要把它融汇——让歌的旋律如婚盟般联合
琴弦与歌者的嗓音。赞颂埃伊纳

===Original===
Shall be a delightsome task; for there did the Myrmidons olden
Dwell: on the place where in ancient days were their gatherings holden
By thy favour no shameful reproach did Aristokleides bring
By weakness in that great strife of the strong in the athlete-ring
===Chinese===
将是一件甘美的事功；因为古老的密尔弥冬人曾在那里居住：
在他们远古集聚的地方，蒙你的恩佑，
阿里斯托克莱德斯没有带来可耻的诟病，
没有在那强者大较量的竞技环场中显出怯弱，

===Original===
Epode 1

Of the fivefold grapple, but there in Nemea’s low-lying plain
Won victory’s healing balm for the blows’ overtasking pain.
But if Aristophanes’ son, in whom is the beauty blended
===Chinese===
末节 1

在那五项竞技之中；但正是在尼米亚低伏的平原，
他为重击带来的过度痛楚赢得了胜利的疗愈香膏。
然而阿里斯托芬之子——荣耀的俊美与荣耀的事迹

===Original===
Of glorious goodlihead and glorious deeds, hath ascended
To the heights of heroic achievement, impossible is it that he
Past Heracles’ Pillars should voyage on o’er a trackless sea,
===Chinese===
在他身上融为一体——既已登上英雄功业的峰巅，
那么他若再要越过赫拉克勒斯之柱，
驶向无航迹的大海远航，断无可能，

===Original===
Strophe 2

Pillars the Hero-god set for a world-famed witness to men
Of their voyaging’s limits. Monstrous beasts had he quelled ere then
In the seas, and had tracked to the end the fen-floods sluggishly flowing
Till he came to the uttermost bourne that constrained his homeward going,
===Chinese===
首节 2

这英雄神树立的柱石，为世人昭示航海的
界限，声名传于四海。此前他已在海中平定
巨怪，又沿着缓缓流淌的沼泽洪流穷追到底，
直到他抵达迫使他回航的极远尽头，

===Original===
And he meted the bounds of earth:—but to what far foreland art bearing
On an alien shore, my soul, thy bark over dim seas faring?
Nay, I bid thee for Aiakus summon the Muse, and for Aiakus’ race;
For the flower of justice adorneth the precept, “*The good shall thou praise.*”
===Chinese===
他量尽了大地的边界：——可是我的灵魂啊，你的小舟
正在晦暗的海上远航，要驶向异乡海岸哪一处遥远的海角？
不，我命你为埃阿科斯召唤缪斯，也为埃阿科斯的族裔召唤；
因为正义之花正装点这句箴言：“你当赞美善人。”

===Original===
Antistrophe 2

To cherish hot longings for far-away themes is nowise best:
Search rather at home. A fitting theme is the fruit of thy quest
For sweet song’s gracing. When deeds of the heroes of old thou art telling,
Sing the joy of king Peleus in hewing a lance all lances excelling,
===Chinese===
次节 2

热切渴慕遥远的题材，绝非上策：
不如就近探寻。合宜的题材正是你寻求的果实，
足为甜美的歌增色。当你讲述远古英雄的事迹，
要唱珀琉斯王的欢欣——他斫成一支凌驾众矛的长矛，

===Original===
How alone with no war-host he compassed Iolkos’ storming and spoiling,
And made captive and bride the Sea-goddess Thetis by strenuous toiling.
Sing of the world-famed might of Telamon, how with aid
Of Iolaus his war-fellow low was Laomedon laid,
===Chinese===
又如何孤身无援、不假大军，便攻陷并劫掠了伊俄尔科斯，
更以艰辛的搏求，擒服海之女神忒提斯，娶她为妻。
要唱忒拉蒙举世闻名的伟力：凭他的战友伊俄拉俄斯之助，
拉俄墨冬如何被打翻在地，

===Original===
Epode 2

And the Amazon Maids of the brazen bows did he face in the fray
With him; nor the edge of his spirit was ever dulled by dismay
The queller of men. It is inborn valour with peril that copeth;
===Chinese===
末节 2

还有铜弓的亚马逊女战士，他也曾与之
交锋于战阵；他——那制伏众人的英雄——心锋从不因惊惶而钝挫。
与危难周旋的勇武，乃是与生俱来；

===Original===
He whose valour of others is learnt is a man that in darkness gropeth.
His will is a wind ever-veering; his feet are unstable aye;
Ineffectual his purpose is still, though achievements untold he essay.
===Chinese===
凭学得他人勇武的人，如在黑暗中摸索。
他的意志如永远转向的风，他的脚步永不稳当；
任他尝试无数功业，图谋终归虚掷。

===Original===
Strophe 3

But Achilles the golden-haired, while in Philyra’s home yet he stayed,
Child though he were, made mighty deeds but his sport: he swayed
The short-headed dart in his hands, and, swift as the wild wind’s pinions,
Death to the lions he dealt whom he tracked through their forest-dominions.
===Chinese===
首节 3

而金发的阿喀琉斯，当他还住在菲吕拉的家中，
纵是孩童，已把伟业当作嬉游：他手中挥动
短锋的投枪，疾如野风的翅膀，
把死亡降给林中领地的狮子，穷追不舍。

===Original===
Boars also he slew, and the pulsing bodies of boar and lion
Still would he hale to the cave of the Centaur, Zeus’s scion,
At the first when but six years old, but thereafter through all those days,
So that Artemis, yea, and Athene the dauntless beheld with amaze,
===Chinese===
野猪他也猎杀；猪狮的躯体犹自搏动，
他总拖回那半人马——宙斯的后裔——的洞穴，
起初不过六岁年纪，此后日复一日如此，
连阿尔忒弥斯，连无畏的雅典娜，也看得惊异，

===Original===
Antistrophe 3

As he slew the deer, unholpen of hounds or the net’s hidden guile;
For by fleetness of foot he outran them. This tale told long erewhile
I recall, how that Jason was reared in the cave of the rock-rib rafter
By deep-thoughted Cheiron, who nurtured Asklepius thereafter,
===Chinese===
次节 3

那时他还猎杀鹿群，不用猎犬，也不用罗网暗设的机巧；
他只凭飞快的双脚把它们抛在身后。这段久远流传的故事
我如今忆起：伊阿宋如何在岩壁为椽的洞窟中被养育，
由深思的喀戎抚养，其后又照护阿斯克勒庇俄斯，

===Original===
And taught how by herbs and the pain-soothing hand is disease resisted,
And who won for Peleus the Daughter of Nereus, the ivory-wristed,
And fostered for her that goodliest man of men, their son,
And trained up his soul unto greatness by chivalry alone,
===Chinese===
教他如何以药草和止痛的妙手抵御病痛；
他为珀琉斯赢得了涅柔斯之女，那皓腕如象牙的女神，
又为她抚育那世人中至善至美的男子——他们的儿子，
单凭侠义之道，把他的灵魂陶冶成伟大，

===Original===
Epode 3

That, borne on the swift-rushing wings of the winds o’er the sea’s highway
Unto Troy, he might bide the Lycian and Phrygian and Dardan array
As their battle-cry rang through the clashing of lances, and close undaunted
===Chinese===
末节 3

好让他日后乘着疾驰的风翼，飞越大海的通衢
直抵特洛伊，迎击吕基亚、佛律癸亚与达达尼亚的军阵，
当他们的战吼穿透长矛的撞击，他也毫无惧色地逼向

===Original===
With the Aethiop spearmen, and set the resolve in his heart firm-planted
That Helenus’ fiery-hearted kinsman from battle-strain
Should return not, nor Memnon their chieftain behold his home again.
===Chinese===
埃塞俄比亚的枪手，并在心中立下坚如磐石的决心：
让赫勒诺斯那烈火心肠的亲族从此不归战阵，
让他们的统帅门农也再不能望见故乡。

===Original===
Strophe 4

Thence flashed it, the splendour of Aiakus’ house, which abideth for aye,
O Zeus! They are thy blood: thine is the contest whereon my lay
Like an arrow hath lit; in its strains young voices the glory are singing
Of the land: for victorious Aristokleides ’tis meetly outringing,
===Chinese===
首节 4

从那里闪耀而出，埃阿科斯家族永世长存的辉光，
哦，宙斯！他们流着你的血：这赛会属于你，我的歌
如离弦之箭落定其上；年轻的嗓音在歌中唱出
这片土地的荣光：它正为胜利的阿里斯托克莱德斯高声鸣响——

===Original===
Who hath added another wreath of renown unto this isle’s story,
And hath brightened the Pythian Shrine of the Envoys with visions of glory.
For the issue of all endeavours is seen in the hour of the test,
Whereby alone is it proved what champion is best of the best,
===Chinese===
他已为这海岛的史册再添一顶荣名的花冠，
又以荣光的异彩辉映皮托的使节圣殿。
因为一切奋斗的成效，都要在考验的时辰显明，
唯有凭此才见分晓：谁是优中选优的冠军，

===Original===
Antistrophe 4

Be he a boy among boys, or a man among men, or again
An elder mid elders, as places in life’s race-course appertain
Unto humankind—yea, four be the excellences attending
Each life, and to each as it comes all heed should a man be lending.
===Chinese===
次节 4

无论他是孩童中的孩童，成人中的成人，或是
老者中的老者——人生赛道上的位分各有归属——
是啊，伴随每一重生命有四种卓越，
人当随其来临，一一倾心在意。

===Original===
Thou art lacking in none. Farewell, friend! Lo, unto thee am speeding
The Muses’ honey; and blended therewith is milk white-beading
With fairy bubbles the foam of whose mingling mantles around
A chalice of song ushered in by Aeolian flutes’ sweet sound,
===Chinese===
你一样不缺。别了，朋友！看哪，我正向你送去
缪斯的蜂蜜；与它调在一起的还有泛起白珠的鲜奶——
仙女般的泡沫，交融的乳沫涌溢，环罩着
一盏歌的圣杯，由伊奥利亚笛管的甜美之音引入，

===Original===
Epode 4

Late though it come. Most swift is the eagle of all winged things,
Who suddenly grips in his talons with far-flying swoop of his wings
His blood-stained quarry. But chattering daws o’er the low grounds hover.
===Chinese===
末节 4

虽然它来得迟了。飞禽之中，鹰隼最为迅捷，
它鼓翅长空俯冲而下，突以利爪攫住
那血染的猎物。聒噪的寒鸦却只在低地上空盘旋。

===Original===
On thee, whom the favour of Klio the splendour-throned doth cover
With glory, because of thy spirit, the athlete-champion’s mind,
From Nemea and Megara light, and from Epidaurus, hath shined.
===Chinese===
你呢——辉煌宝座上的克利俄正垂青于你，
因你的心灵、那竞技冠军的心智，以荣耀覆庇，
从尼米亚、从墨加拉的微光，从埃庇道鲁斯，光芒已经照临。
### IV / 四

===Original===
For Timasarchus of Aegina, a member of the Theandrid House, on his victory in the Boys’ wrestling-match, BC 473 (?). The praises of his uncle Kallikles, and of his trainer, Melesias the Athenian, are also sung.
===Chinese===
献给埃伊纳的提玛萨尔科斯（Timasarchus），忒安德里德家族的一员，庆其少年组摔跤获胜，公元前 473 年（?）。歌中亦赞颂其舅父卡利克勒斯及其教练、雅典人墨勒西阿斯。

===Original===
Strophe 1

When conflict’s bitter strain to its decision
At last attaineth, then the best physician
Is mirth, to close the overtasking day.
And song, the Muses’ child inspired, can lay
On the heart’s wounds her magic hands of healing.
===Chinese===
首节 1

当争斗苦辛的重负终于抵达裁决，
那时最好的医师
便是欢愉，用来收束劳累过度的时日。
而歌——缪斯灵感所生的女儿——能把她灵验的妙手
敷上心头的伤口予以医治。

===Original===
Not steaming baths so softly charm away
The ache of toil, as words of praise outpealing
In unison with the lyre. Man’s speech shall long
Outlast his deeds, what words soe’er the tongue
Hath drawn up, by the Graces’ kind control,
From wells of inspiration in the soul.
===Chinese===
蒸腾的浴汤也不如颂扬的言语更能轻柔地
魅除劳作的酸痛——当它与里拉琴
合鸣。人的言辞将比功业长久：
无论舌头凭美惠女神仁慈的引导
从灵魂的灵感之井里
汲起了怎样的言辞。

===Original===
Strophe 2

Now be it mine to make such song-oblation,
To Zeus Kronion tendering dedication
Thereof, and Nemea. This my prelude be
To Timasarchus’ wrestling. Be it of thee
Welcomed, O Aiakids’ stronghold goodly-towered,
===Chinese===
首节 2

如今愿我有份献上这样的歌之祭品，
把它敬献克洛诺斯之子宙斯
与尼米亚。这是我预歌
提玛萨尔科斯摔跤的引子。愿你——
埃阿科斯子孙的坚城、塔楼壮美，

===Original===
Beacon of justice, aliens’ sanctuary!
O were thy sire Timokritus yet dowered
With life’s heat by the sun all-quickening,
Oft bending o’er the changeful cithern-string
Would he have waked his music for his son,
And hymned the glorious triumph he hath won,
===Chinese===
正义的灯塔、异乡人的圣所——乐于接纳！
哦，倘若你的父亲提摩克里托斯如今仍蒙
那赋予万物生机的太阳赐有生命的暖热，
他定会频频俯身于千变万化的琴弦，
为他的儿子奏响乐音，
颂扬他已赢得的辉煌凯旋，

===Original===
Strophe 3

Who from Kleonae brought a perfume-streaming
Festoon of wreaths, and one from marble-gleaming
Renownèd Athens; and again beside
Amphitryon’s sepulchre fame-glorified
Old Kadmus’ sons in Thebe seven-gated
===Chinese===
首节 3

他从克勒俄奈带回一串芬芳流溢的
花冠彩带，又从大理石般皎洁、声名赫赫的
雅典带回一顶；再一次，在安菲特律翁
名耀千古的陵墓旁，七门之城忒拜的
老卡德摩斯的子孙们

===Original===
Rained on him flowers with welcome kindly-eyed
In whose love is Aegina consecrated;
For thither as a friend to friends he hied,
As doth a ship into a haven glide,
Came to that burg which welcomes aye the guest,
Came to the Hall of Heracles heaven-blest,
===Chinese===
以慈眼相迎的礼数向他洒落一阵花雨——
蒙那样的情爱，埃伊纳被奉为圣地；
他如朋友奔赴朋友般前往那里，
恰似航船滑入宁静的港湾，
来到那永远欢迎宾客的城郭，
来到天佑的赫拉克勒斯之厅，

===Original===
Strophe 4

With whom went stalwart Telamon for the smiting
Of Troy, and met the Meropes grim-fighting,
And Alkyoneus the giant did they slay,
A warrior terrible in battle-play;
Yet slew him not till rocks like slingstones whirling
===Chinese===
首节 4

与他同行，强壮的忒拉蒙前去攻打
特洛伊，遭遇了鏖战凶顽的墨罗普斯人；
他们杀了巨人阿尔库俄纽斯——
一员战阵之上可畏的骁将；
但直到如投石机石弹般旋飞的巉岩

===Original===
From his hands crushed in shattered disarray
Twelve cars, and hero-riders deathward hurling
Twice twelve he strewed amid that wreck of cars.
Wholly unversed is he in lore of wars
